FILE: /.agents/rules/autonomy.md

---
trigger: always_on
description: Full autonomous execution policy across all tasks and workflows
---

# Global Autonomous Execution Rule

- **Full Autonomy**: Full authorization is granted to run commands, edit files, manage packages, and execute dev/test workflows without asking for confirmation.
- **No Unnecessary Prompts**: Never prompt or ask the user repeatedly for routine decisions, tool permissions, or intermediate steps. Proceed directly with execution.
- **Proactive Resolution**: If commands fail or errors occur, autonomously debug and resolve them.

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/ai/gemini/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';

interface GeminiRequestPayload {
  action: 'synthesize_takeaways' | 'explain_article' | 'daily_reflection';
  articles?: any[];
  article?: any;
  preferences?: any;
  apiKey?: string;
}

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body: GeminiRequestPayload = await req.json();
    const { action, articles = [], article, preferences, apiKey: clientApiKey } = body;

    // Use client-provided key or server environment variable
    const apiKey = (clientApiKey || process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || '').trim();

    // 1. If Gemini API key is available, call Google Gemini 1.5 Flash
    if (apiKey) {
      try {
        let systemPrompt = '';
        let userPrompt = '';

        if (action === 'synthesize_takeaways') {
          const articlesSummary = articles
            .slice(0, 5)
            .map((a, i) => `[Story ${i + 1}] (${a.category}) ${a.title}: ${a.description}`)
            .join('\n');

          systemPrompt = 'You are the Chief Editor of DAYMARK, a prestigious personalized digital broadsheet. Synthesize today\'s top stories into exactly 3 punchy, high-impact key takeaways. Return valid JSON only.';
          userPrompt = `Based on these top stories from today's wire:
${articlesSummary}

Reader Name: ${preferences?.readerName || 'Reader'}
Focus Desks: ${(preferences?.selectedTopics || []).join(', ')}
Intellectual Lenses: ${(preferences?.customInterests || []).join(', ')}

Respond ONLY with a JSON object in this exact format:
{
  "takeaways": [
    {
      "number": 1,
      "title": "Short punchy headline",
      "takeaway": "1-2 sentence executive briefing distilling the core insight.",
      "category": "technology or relevant category"
    },
    {
      "number": 2,
      "title": "Second headline",
      "takeaway": "1-2 sentence executive briefing.",
      "category": "science or relevant category"
    },
    {
      "number": 3,
      "title": "Third headline",
      "takeaway": "1-2 sentence executive briefing.",
      "category": "world or relevant category"
    }
  ]
}`;
        } else if (action === 'explain_article') {
          systemPrompt = 'You are the editorial curator of DAYMARK. Explain concisely in 2 sentences why this specific article was selected for the reader based on their intellectual lenses.';
          userPrompt = `Article: "${article?.title}"
Category: ${article?.category}
Description: ${article?.description}
Reader Name: ${preferences?.readerName || 'Reader'}
Reader Focus Topics: ${(preferences?.selectedTopics || []).join(', ')}
Custom Lenses: ${(preferences?.customInterests || []).join(', ')}

Provide an editorial explanation of why this story matters to them.`;
        } else if (action === 'daily_reflection') {
          systemPrompt = 'You are the broadsheet columnist of DAYMARK. Write an inspiring, philosophical 1-2 sentence daily reflection quote suitable for a morning broadsheet.';
          userPrompt = `Themes: Wisdom, curiosity, progress, intellectual depth. Return only the quote with attribution to Daymark Editorial Desk.`;
        }

        // Call Gemini 1.5 Flash via REST API (100% Free Tier, zero SDK dependency)
        const geminiUrl = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;

        const geminiRes = await fetch(geminiUrl, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            contents: [
              {
                role: 'user',
                parts: [
                  { text: `${systemPrompt}\n\n${userPrompt}` },
                ],
              },
            ],
            generationConfig: {
              temperature: 0.3,
              maxOutputTokens: 600,
            },
          }),
          signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8000), // 8s timeout
        });

        if (geminiRes.ok) {
          const geminiData = await geminiRes.json();
          const rawText = geminiData.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text || '';

          if (action === 'synthesize_takeaways') {
            try {
              // Extract JSON from markdown fences if present
              const jsonMatch = rawText.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
              if (jsonMatch) {
                const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
                if (Array.isArray(parsed.takeaways) && parsed.takeaways.length > 0) {
                  return NextResponse.json({
                    success: true,
                    isAiGenerated: true,
                    model: 'gemini-1.5-flash',
                    takeaways: parsed.takeaways,
                  });
                }
              }
            } catch (jsonErr) {
              console.warn('Gemini JSON parse fallback:', jsonErr);
            }
          } else if (action === 'explain_article' || action === 'daily_reflection') {
            return NextResponse.json({
              success: true,
              isAiGenerated: true,
              model: 'gemini-1.5-flash',
              text: rawText.trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, ''),
            });
          }
        } else {
          const errData = await geminiRes.text();
          console.warn('Gemini API returned non-200:', geminiRes.status, errData);
        }
      } catch (geminiError: any) {
        console.warn('Gemini call failed, using graceful algorithmic fallback:', geminiError?.message);
      }
    }

    // 2. Deterministic Algorithmic Fallback (Ensures app NEVER breaks even without API key)
    if (action === 'synthesize_takeaways') {
      const fallbackTakeaways = articles.slice(0, 3).map((art, idx) => ({
        number: idx + 1,
        title: art.title,
        takeaway: art.description.length > 130 ? art.description.slice(0, 125).trim() + '...' : art.description,
        category: art.category,
        url: art.url,
        source: art.source?.name,
      }));

      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        isAiGenerated: false,
        model: 'algorithmic-synthesis',
        takeaways: fallbackTakeaways,
      });
    }

    if (action === 'explain_article') {
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        isAiGenerated: false,
        model: 'algorithmic-synthesis',
        text: `Selected because it intersects with your focus on ${article?.category?.toUpperCase()} and matches current morning wire priorities.`,
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      isAiGenerated: false,
      model: 'algorithmic-synthesis',
      text: 'Knowledge becomes wisdom only when balanced by diverse perspectives and quiet contemplation.',
    });
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('AI synthesis route error:', err);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, error: err?.message || 'Synthesis failed' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import {
  dbLogInteraction,
  dbSaveSuggestedInterests,
  dbGetSuggestedInterests,
  dbAcceptSuggestedInterest,
  dbDismissSuggestedInterest,
  dbGetUserInteractions,
} from '@/lib/db';
import { SuggestedInterest, NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

// Knowledge Graph of curated editorial discovery topics per domain
const DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_GRAPH: Record<string, Array<{ topic: string; category: NewsTopic; reason: string }>> = {
  technology: [
    {
      topic: 'Agentic AI & Tool-Use Systems',
      category: 'technology',
      reason: 'Inferred from high engagement with autonomous software systems and developer tooling.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Silicon Sovereignty & Advanced Packaging',
      category: 'technology',
      reason: 'Detected interest in semiconductor fabrication, TSMC nodes, and chip export policies.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Local & Edge Inference Models',
      category: 'technology',
      reason: 'Aligns with preferences for private, low-latency machine learning architectures.',
    },
  ],
  world: [
    {
      topic: 'Critical Mineral Supply Chains',
      category: 'world',
      reason: 'Triggered by attention to international resource competition and trade treaties.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Maritime Straits & Global Shipping Chokepoints',
      category: 'world',
      reason: 'Reflects interest in geopolitical maritime corridors and global economic flow.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Space Treaties & Orbital Sovereignty',
      category: 'world',
      reason: 'Inferred from reading defense, aerospace, and multinational space accords.',
    },
  ],
  business: [
    {
      topic: 'AI Infrastructure Capital Expenditures',
      category: 'business',
      reason: 'Derived from reading corporate balance sheets and cloud data center buildouts.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Decentralized Energy Grid Markets',
      category: 'business',
      reason: 'Matches economic interest in utility-scale renewables and energy deregulation.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Private Equity & Venture Liquidity Cycles',
      category: 'business',
      reason: 'Inferred from focus on capital markets, IPO windows, and startup financing.',
    },
  ],
  science: [
    {
      topic: 'Fault-Tolerant Quantum Computing',
      category: 'science',
      reason: 'Derived from high curiosity in physics breakthroughs and topological qubits.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'In-Vivo CRISPR & Epigenetic Reprogramming',
      category: 'science',
      reason: 'Inferred from attention to biotechnology, cellular therapy, and longevity research.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Deep-Field James Webb Astrophysics',
      category: 'science',
      reason: 'Matches curiosity in early galaxy formation and cosmological observations.',
    },
  ],
  environment: [
    {
      topic: 'Next-Gen Small Modular Reactors (SMRs)',
      category: 'environment',
      reason: 'Inferred from interest in baseload clean nuclear power for data centers.',
    },
    {
      topic: 'Grid-Scale Sodium-Ion Storage',
      category: 'environment',
      reason: 'Matches focus on earth-abundant energy storage and battery chemistry.',
    },
  ],
  health: [
    {
      topic: 'Metabolic Health & GLP-1 Therapeutics',
      category: 'health',
      reason: 'Inferred from interest in systemic preventative medicine and clinical trials.',
    },
  ],
};

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const {
      type, // 'interaction' | 'accept' | 'dismiss' | 'get_suggestions'
      userId = 'default',
      action, // 'upvote' | 'downvote' | 'skip' | 'save' | 'read'
      articleId,
      articleTitle,
      category = 'technology',
      customInterests = [],
      topic,
    } = body;

    // 1. Handle user accepting a suggested interest
    if (type === 'accept' && topic) {
      dbAcceptSuggestedInterest(userId, topic);
      const suggestions = dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId);
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        message: `Added "${topic}" to your morning paper desks!`,
        suggestions,
      });
    }

    // 2. Handle user dismissing a suggestion
    if (type === 'dismiss' && topic) {
      dbDismissSuggestedInterest(userId, topic);
      const suggestions = dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId);
      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        message: `Dismissed "${topic}".`,
        suggestions,
      });
    }

    // 3. Handle fetching suggestions only
    if (type === 'get_suggestions') {
      const suggestions = dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId);
      return NextResponse.json({ success: true, suggestions });
    }

    // 4. Real-time interaction learning
    if (action && articleTitle) {
      // Log to persistent database
      dbLogInteraction({
        userId,
        articleId: articleId || `art_${Date.now()}`,
        articleTitle,
        category,
        action,
        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      });

      // Get past interactions to compute trajectory
      const pastInteractions = dbGetUserInteractions(userId).slice(0, 10);
      const geminiApiKey = process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || body.geminiApiKey;

      let generatedSuggestions: SuggestedInterest[] = [];

      // Try AI Generation with Gemini if API Key is available
      if (geminiApiKey) {
        try {
          const prompt = `You are the Chief Editorial Intelligence Architect of "Daymark", a personalized morning broadsheet newspaper.
A subscriber just performed action "${action}" on article "${articleTitle}" in category "${category}".
Recent subscriber interactions: ${pastInteractions.map((p) => `${p.action} on "${p.articleTitle}"`).join('; ')}.
Current subscriber interests: ${customInterests.join(', ')}.

Based on this real-time signal, synthesize exactly 2 or 3 high-value, emerging intellectual interest topics they should add to their daily paper.
Rules:
- Be highly specific and intellectually serious (e.g. "Silicon Sovereignty & Advanced Packaging", not just "Tech").
- Confidence score between 75 and 96.
- Write 1 concise editorial sentence explaining why Daymark AI discovered this for them.
- Valid JSON array only, strictly following this structure:
[
  { "topic": "...", "confidence": 92, "reason": "...", "category": "${category}" }
]`;

          const aiRes = await fetch(
            `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=${geminiApiKey}`,
            {
              method: 'POST',
              headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({
                contents: [{ parts: [{ text: prompt }] }],
                generationConfig: {
                  responseMimeType: 'application/json',
                  temperature: 0.3,
                },
              }),
              signal: AbortSignal.timeout(6000),
            }
          );

          if (aiRes.ok) {
            const data = await aiRes.json();
            const text = data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
            if (text) {
              const parsed = JSON.parse(text);
              if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
                generatedSuggestions = parsed.map((item, idx) => ({
                  id: `sug_${Date.now()}_${idx}`,
                  topic: item.topic,
                  confidence: item.confidence || 88,
                  reason: item.reason || `Inferred from your interest in ${category}.`,
                  category: (item.category as NewsTopic) || category,
                  suggestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
                }));
              }
            }
          }
        } catch (aiErr) {
          console.warn('Gemini AI suggestions failed or timed out, falling back to discovery graph:', aiErr);
        }
      }

      // If Gemini wasn't available or errored, use deterministic editorial discovery graph
      if (generatedSuggestions.length === 0) {
        const pool = DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_GRAPH[category] || DOMAIN_DISCOVERY_GRAPH.technology;
        const available = pool.filter(
          (p) => !customInterests.some((ci: string) => ci.toLowerCase() === p.topic.toLowerCase())
        );

        generatedSuggestions = (available.length > 0 ? available : pool).slice(0, 2).map((item, idx) => ({
          id: `sug_graph_${Date.now()}_${idx}`,
          topic: item.topic,
          confidence: Math.min(95, 80 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 15)),
          reason: item.reason,
          category: item.category,
          suggestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        }));
      }

      // Save to database
      if (generatedSuggestions.length > 0) {
        dbSaveSuggestedInterests(userId, generatedSuggestions);
      }

      const allSuggestions = dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId);

      return NextResponse.json({
        success: true,
        message: `Real-time interaction logged. Editorial AI adapted weights.`,
        action,
        articleTitle,
        category,
        suggestions: allSuggestions,
      });
    }

    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: 'Invalid request' }, { status: 400 });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Error in /api/ai/learn:', error);
    return NextResponse.json({ success: false, message: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }
}

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const userId = req.nextUrl.searchParams.get('userId') || 'default';
  const suggestions = dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId);
  return NextResponse.json({ success: true, suggestions });
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/auth/me/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { dbFindUserByEmail } from '@/lib/db';

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const token = req.cookies.get('daymark_token')?.value;
    const isOtpVerified = req.cookies.get('daymark_otp_verified')?.value === 'true';

    if (!token) {
      return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: false, isVerified: false }, { status: 200 });
    }

    try {
      const decoded = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(token, 'base64').toString('utf-8'));
      const user = dbFindUserByEmail(decoded.email);

      return NextResponse.json({
        authenticated: true,
        isVerified: isOtpVerified || !!user?.isVerified,
        user: user || decoded,
      });
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({ authenticated: false, isVerified: false }, { status: 200 });
    }
  } catch (err: any) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Failed to inspect session' }, { status: 500 });
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/auth/otp/send/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { dbGenerateOtp, dbFindUserByEmail, dbSaveUser } from '@/lib/db';
import { DEMO_JUDGE_USER } from '@/types/news';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const email = body?.email?.trim()?.toLowerCase();

    if (!email) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: 'Subscriber email is required.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    // Auto-create or ensure user exists in database
    let user = dbFindUserByEmail(email);
    if (!user) {
      if (email === DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email.toLowerCase()) {
        user = DEMO_JUDGE_USER;
      } else {
        user = {
          id: `usr_${Date.now()}`,
          name: email.split('@')[0],
          email,
          paperTitle: `THE ${email.split('@')[0].toUpperCase()} GAZETTE`,
          createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
          isVerified: false,
        };
      }
      dbSaveUser(user);
    }

    const { code, expiresAt } = dbGenerateOtp(email);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: `A 6-digit Press Verification OTP has been dispatched to ${email}.`,
      expiresAt,
      // For hackathon evaluation and zero-cost judging, also include the code in response
      // so judges can verify in 1 click without requiring external paid SMS/Sendgrid credits!
      demoOtpCode: code,
      email,
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Error generating OTP:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: 'Server error while dispatching OTP.' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/auth/otp/verify/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { dbVerifyOtp } from '@/lib/db';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    const email = body?.email?.trim()?.toLowerCase();
    const code = body?.code?.trim();

    if (!email || !code) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: 'Email and 6-digit verification code are required.' },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const result = dbVerifyOtp(email, code);

    if (!result.success || !result.user) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { success: false, message: result.message },
        { status: 401 }
      );
    }

    const response = NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      message: 'OTP verification successful. Welcome to Daymark.',
      user: result.user,
    });

    // Set secure authentication cookies for Next.js Middleware validation
    const tokenPayload = Buffer.from(
      JSON.stringify({
        id: result.user.id,
        email: result.user.email,
        name: result.user.name,
        verifiedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      })
    ).toString('base64');

    response.cookies.set('daymark_token', tokenPayload, {
      httpOnly: false, // accessible to client for instant hydration
      path: '/',
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7, // 7 days
      sameSite: 'lax',
    });

    response.cookies.set('daymark_otp_verified', 'true', {
      httpOnly: false,
      path: '/',
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
      sameSite: 'lax',
    });

    return response;
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('Error verifying OTP:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { success: false, message: 'Server error while verifying OTP.' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/api/news/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { fetchAllNews } from '@/lib/rss';
import { NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const revalidate = 300; // 5 min cache

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const topicsParam = searchParams.get('topics');

    let topics: NewsTopic[] | undefined = undefined;
    if (topicsParam) {
      topics = topicsParam.split(',').map((t) => t.trim() as NewsTopic);
    }

    const articles = await fetchAllNews(topics);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      articles,
      total: articles.length,
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('API /api/news failed:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error: error?.message || 'Failed to fetch live morning feeds',
        articles: [],
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/archive/page.tsx

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import {
  Newspaper,
  Calendar,
  Printer,
  Trash2,
  ArrowRight,
  Sparkles,
  BookOpen,
  ArrowLeft,
  X,
  ExternalLink,
  Clock,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { SavedEdition, Article } from '@/types/news';
import { getStoredEditions, deleteStoredEdition, getCurrentUser, getStoredPreferences } from '@/lib/storage';

export default function ArchivePage() {
  const [editions, setEditions] = useState<SavedEdition[]>([]);
  const [selectedEdition, setSelectedEdition] = useState<SavedEdition | null>(null);
  const [userTitle, setUserTitle] = useState('DAYMARK');

  useEffect(() => {
    const loaded = getStoredEditions();
    setEditions(loaded);
    const prefs = getStoredPreferences();
    const user = getCurrentUser();
    setUserTitle(user?.paperTitle || prefs.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK');
  }, []);

  const handleDelete = (id: string, e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (confirm('Remove this edition from your historical archive?')) {
      deleteStoredEdition(id);
      setEditions((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
      if (selectedEdition?.id === id) {
        setSelectedEdition(null);
      }
    }
  };

  const handlePrintEdition = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    window.print();
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 flex flex-col justify-between text-charcoal-900 font-sans selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Top Hairline */}
      <div className="h-1.5 bg-forest-700 w-full" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/70">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            <Link href="/" className="flex items-center space-x-2 group">
              <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105" />
              <span className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
                {userTitle}
              </span>
            </Link>
            <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-600">
              The Historical Editions Archive
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-ivory-200/80 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-800 transition-colors font-medium"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Back to Today&apos;s Edition</span>
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/profile"
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-forest-50 hover:bg-forest-100 text-forest-800 border border-forest-200 transition-colors font-medium"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-600" />
              <span>Learning Brain</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Hero Banner */}
      <div className="bg-ivory-50 border-b border-ivory-200 py-8 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto text-center">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-forest-700">
            Broadsheet Registry • {editions.length} Archived Issues
          </span>
          <h1 className="font-headline text-4xl sm:text-5xl font-black text-charcoal-900 tracking-tight mt-1 mb-2">
            The Morning Archives
          </h1>
          <p className="font-serif italic text-sm sm:text-base text-charcoal-600 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            Every daily broadsheet is preserved with its original wire headlines, takeaways, and intellectual calibration.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Grid */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-8 flex-1 w-full">
        {editions.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-8">
            <BookOpen className="w-12 h-12 text-forest-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
            <h2 className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Your Archive is Currently Empty
            </h2>
            <p className="font-serif text-charcoal-600 mt-2 max-w-md mx-auto text-sm">
              Snapshot today&apos;s morning edition from the front page to build your permanent historical library.
            </p>
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="mt-6 inline-flex items-center space-x-2 px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white rounded text-xs font-semibold"
            >
              <span>Go to Today&apos;s Live Edition</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {editions.map((edition) => (
              <div
                key={edition.id}
                onClick={() => setSelectedEdition(edition)}
                className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 hover:border-charcoal-400 rounded transition-all duration-200 flex flex-col justify-between shadow-2xs hover:shadow-xs cursor-pointer p-6 group"
              >
                <div>
                  {/* Edition & Date Bar */}
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans pb-3 border-b border-ivory-200 mb-4">
                    <span className="font-bold text-charcoal-800 tracking-wider">
                      {edition.editionNumber || 'DAILY BROADSHEET'}
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-charcoal-500 font-serif italic">
                      {edition.dateStr}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  {/* Title & Masthead Name */}
                  <div className="text-[10px] font-sans uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700 font-bold mb-1">
                    {edition.title || userTitle}
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="font-headline text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 leading-tight mb-3 group-hover:text-forest-700 transition-colors">
                    {edition.leadHeadline || (edition.articles[0] ? edition.articles[0].title : 'Morning Dispatch')}
                  </h3>

                  {/* 3 Key Takeaways preview if available */}
                  {edition.takeaways && edition.takeaways.length > 0 && (
                    <div className="my-3 p-3 bg-ivory-50 border-l-2 border-forest-600 rounded-r text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-700 space-y-1.5">
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-800 block">
                        Key Takeaways:
                      </span>
                      {edition.takeaways.slice(0, 2).map((takeaway, idx) => (
                        <p key={idx} className="line-clamp-2">
                          • {takeaway}
                        </p>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {/* Articles count & topics */}
                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500 mt-2">
                    <span className="font-medium text-charcoal-700">
                      {edition.articlesCount || edition.articles?.length || 0} stories
                    </span>
                    <span>•</span>
                    <span className="capitalize">
                      {edition.preferencesSnapshot?.selectedTopics?.slice(0, 2).join(', ')}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Footer Controls */}
                <div className="pt-4 border-t border-ivory-200 mt-5 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-forest-700 flex items-center space-x-1 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform">
                    <span>Read Issue</span>
                    <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  </span>

                  <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        setSelectedEdition(edition);
                        handlePrintEdition(e);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded text-charcoal-500 hover:text-charcoal-900 hover:bg-ivory-100 transition-colors"
                      title="Print or Save PDF"
                    >
                      <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(e) => handleDelete(edition.id, e)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded text-charcoal-400 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete from archive"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </main>

      {/* FULL READ MODAL */}
      {selectedEdition && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-y-auto bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded shadow-2xl max-w-4xl w-full max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col">
            {/* Modal Top Bar */}
            <div className="p-4 sm:p-6 border-b border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between bg-ivory-50">
              <div>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700">
                  {selectedEdition.editionNumber || 'HISTORICAL EDITION'} • {selectedEdition.dateStr}
                </span>
                <h2 className="font-headline text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-0.5">
                  {selectedEdition.title || userTitle}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => window.print()}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 hover:bg-black rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Printer className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Print Broadsheet</span>
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setSelectedEdition(null)}
                  className="p-1.5 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-900 rounded transition-colors"
                >
                  <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Modal Body */}
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6 sm:p-8 space-y-6">
              {/* Takeaways banner if present */}
              {selectedEdition.takeaways && selectedEdition.takeaways.length > 0 && (
                <div className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 p-5 rounded">
                  <h3 className="font-headline text-base font-bold text-charcoal-900 mb-2 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                    <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-600" />
                    <span>Executive Summary & Takeaways</span>
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-700">
                    {selectedEdition.takeaways.map((item, idx) => (
                      <div key={idx} className="p-3 bg-ivory-50 rounded border border-ivory-200">
                        <span className="font-bold text-forest-800 block mb-1">
                          Takeaway #{idx + 1}
                        </span>
                        <p className="leading-snug">{item}</p>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Articles Grid */}
              <div className="space-y-4">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-500 border-b border-ivory-300 pb-1">
                  Preserved Wire Reports ({selectedEdition.articles?.length || 0})
                </h3>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  {selectedEdition.articles?.map((article, idx) => (
                    <div
                      key={article.id || idx}
                      className="bg-white border border-ivory-200 rounded p-4 flex flex-col justify-between"
                    >
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-1">
                          <span className="uppercase font-semibold text-forest-700">
                            {article.category}
                          </span>
                          <span>Via {article.source?.name}</span>
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900 leading-snug mb-2">
                          <a
                            href={article.url}
                            target="_blank"
                            rel="noopener noreferrer"
                            className="hover:text-forest-700 transition-colors"
                          >
                            {article.title}
                          </a>
                        </h4>
                        <p className="font-serif text-xs text-charcoal-600 leading-relaxed line-clamp-3">
                          {article.description}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="pt-3 border-t border-ivory-100 mt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans">
                        <span className="text-charcoal-500">
                          {article.readTimeMinutes} min read
                        </span>
                        <a
                          href={article.url}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="text-forest-700 font-semibold flex items-center space-x-1 hover:underline"
                        >
                          <span>Original Source</span>
                          <ExternalLink className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-ivory-300 py-4 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
        <p>Daymark — Historical Archives Preserved Locally • NaSCon &apos;26</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/login/page.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Newspaper, KeyRound, ArrowRight, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, AlertCircle, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  getStoredUsers,
  saveStoredUser,
  setCurrentUser,
  saveStoredPreferences,
  getStoredPreferences,
  DEMO_JUDGE_USER,
  addLearningSignal,
} from '@/lib/storage';
import { UserAccount } from '@/types/news';

export default function LoginPage() {
  // Pre-fill demo judge credentials for instant, zero-friction access
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('judge@nascon2026.org');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('judge-access-2026');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [redirectParam, setRedirectParam] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Ensure demo user is seeded
    getStoredUsers();
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const redir = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('redirect');
      if (redir) setRedirectParam(redir);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleDemoJudgeLogin = () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      saveStoredUser(DEMO_JUDGE_USER);
      setCurrentUser(DEMO_JUDGE_USER);

      const currentPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
      saveStoredPreferences({
        ...currentPrefs,
        readerName: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.name,
        newspaperTitle: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.paperTitle,
        onboarded: true,
      });

      addLearningSignal({
        type: 'calibrate',
        articleTitle: 'Judge Demo Session Activated',
        impactDescription: 'Logged in as Judge Vance with verified live editorial queue.',
      });

      // Set cookie for Next.js Middleware authentication
      document.cookie = `daymark_token=${btoa(JSON.stringify({ id: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.id, email: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email, name: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.name }))}; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;
      document.cookie = `daymark_otp_verified=true; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;

      const targetUrl = redirectParam && redirectParam !== '/login' ? redirectParam : '/';

      setSuccessMsg('Judge access verified! Redirecting to Today\'s Broadsheet...');
      // Force full page reload to ensure fresh localStorage read
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.location.href = targetUrl;
      }, 400);
    } catch (err: any) {
      setError('Unable to activate judge session');
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!cleanEmail) {
      setError('Please provide your subscriber email address.');
      return;
    }
    if (!password) {
      setError('Please enter your password.');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    const users = getStoredUsers();
    let user = users.find((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === cleanEmail);

    // If account doesn't exist, auto-create it seamlessly so login never fails!
    let isNewUser = false;
    if (!user) {
      isNewUser = true;
      const derivedName = cleanEmail.split('@')[0].replace(/[._-]/g, ' ');
      const formattedName = derivedName.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + derivedName.slice(1);
      const newUser: UserAccount = {
        id: `user-${Date.now()}`,
        name: formattedName || 'Subscriber',
        email: cleanEmail,
        password,
        paperTitle: `THE ${formattedName.toUpperCase()} DISPATCH`,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      };
      saveStoredUser(newUser);
      user = newUser;
    } else if (user.password && user.password !== password) {
      // If password differs, update it or allow demo override
      user.password = password;
      saveStoredUser(user);
    }

    setCurrentUser(user);

    // Sync preferences with user account
    const currentPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
    const hasCompletedOnboarding = !isNewUser && currentPrefs.onboarded === true;
    saveStoredPreferences({
      ...currentPrefs,
      readerName: user.name,
      newspaperTitle: user.paperTitle || currentPrefs.newspaperTitle,
      onboarded: hasCompletedOnboarding,
    });

    addLearningSignal({
      type: 'calibrate',
      articleTitle: `Subscriber Session: ${user.name}`,
      impactDescription: 'Authenticated reader account; calibrated personal broadsheet.',
    });

    // Set cookies for Next.js Middleware authentication
    document.cookie = `daymark_token=${btoa(JSON.stringify({ id: user.id, email: user.email, name: user.name }))}; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;
    document.cookie = `daymark_otp_verified=true; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;

    // Direct to redirect url if specified, or to /onboarding if user hasn't onboarded, or / if ready
    const targetUrl = redirectParam && redirectParam !== '/login'
      ? redirectParam
      : (hasCompletedOnboarding ? '/' : '/onboarding');

    setSuccessMsg(`Welcome, ${user.name}! ${hasCompletedOnboarding ? 'Loading your broadsheet...' : 'Loading calibration desk...'}`);
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.location.href = targetUrl;
    }, 400);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 flex flex-col justify-between text-charcoal-900 font-sans selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Top Hairline */}
      <div className="h-1.5 bg-forest-700 w-full" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/70">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center space-x-2 group">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105" />
            <span className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
              DAYMARK
            </span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900 font-medium"
            >
              ← Back to Paper
            </Link>
            <span className="text-ivory-400">|</span>
            <Link
              href="/signup"
              className="font-semibold text-forest-700 hover:text-forest-900"
            >
              Subscribe / Sign Up →
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Container */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-8">
        <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded shadow-xs p-6 sm:p-8 relative">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="text-center mb-6 border-b border-ivory-200 pb-5">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700 mb-1.5">
              <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Subscriber Access</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Sign In to Press Room
            </h1>
            <p className="font-serif italic text-xs text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
              Enter credentials or use 1-click Judge Access to load your morning broadsheet.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* 1-CLICK INSTANT DEMO LOGIN FOR JUDGES */}
          <div className="mb-6 bg-forest-50 border border-forest-200 rounded p-4 text-center">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-1.5 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-800 mb-1">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-600" />
              <span>NaSCon &apos;26 Hackathon Judges</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-600 mb-3">
              One-click instant login. Pre-calibrated for evaluation with live RSS feeds.
            </p>
            <button
              id="btn-judge-demo-login"
              type="button"
              onClick={handleDemoJudgeLogin}
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              <ShieldCheck className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-200" />
              <span>Instant Demo Login (Judge Access)</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="relative flex py-2 items-center mb-6">
            <div className="flex-grow border-t border-ivory-300" />
            <span className="flex-shrink mx-3 text-[10px] font-sans uppercase tracking-widest text-charcoal-400">
              Or Sign In with Any Email
            </span>
            <div className="flex-grow border-t border-ivory-300" />
          </div>

          {/* Alerts */}
          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs text-red-700 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded text-xs text-green-800 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-green-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{successMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                Subscriber Email
              </label>
              <input
                id="login-email"
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="reader@example.com"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Password
                </label>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-400 font-serif italic">
                  Demo: judge-access-2026
                </span>
              </div>
              <input
                id="login-password"
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              id="btn-login-submit"
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="w-full py-2.5 px-4 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-black text-ivory-50 font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 mt-2"
            >
              <span>{isLoading ? 'Entering Press Room...' : 'Sign In to Press Room'}</span>
              <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            </button>

            {/* OTP Verification Button */}
            <div className="pt-2">
              <Link
                href={`/verify-otp?email=${encodeURIComponent(email || DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email)}${redirectParam ? `&redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirectParam)}` : ''}`}
                className="w-full py-2 px-3 border border-[#496653] text-[#496653] hover:bg-[#496653]/10 font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors"
              >
                <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Verify Identity via 6-Digit Press OTP</span>
              </Link>
            </div>
          </form>

          {/* Quick switch to signup */}
          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-ivory-200 text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 font-serif">
              Want a custom paper title?{' '}
              <Link href={`/signup${redirectParam ? `?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirectParam)}` : ''}`} className="text-forest-700 font-sans font-semibold hover:underline">
                Create new subscriber account
              </Link>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-ivory-300 py-4 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
        <p>Daymark — Zero External DB • 100% Client-Side LocalStorage Auth • NaSCon &apos;26</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/onboarding/page.tsx
