'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import {
  Compass,
  Check,
  Plus,
  X,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowRight,
  Clock,
  BookOpen,
  Newspaper,
  TrendingUp,
  Cpu,
  Globe,
  Atom,
  HeartPulse,
  Leaf,
  PenTool,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS, DEFAULT_PREFERENCES } from '@/types/news';
import { getStoredPreferences, saveStoredPreferences, getCurrentUser, addLearningSignal } from '@/lib/storage';

const INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS = [
  'Artificial Intelligence',
  'Space Exploration',
  'Clean Energy',
  'Quantum Computing',
  'Global Geopolitics',
  'Semiconductors & Chips',
  'Biotechnology',
  'Electric Vehicles',
  'Venture Capital',
  'Cybersecurity',
  'Macroeconomics',
  'Neuroscience',
];

export default function OnboardingPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [step, setStep] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(1);
  const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState<UserPreferences>(DEFAULT_PREFERENCES);
  const [readerName, setReaderName] = useState('');
  const [newspaperTitle, setNewspaperTitle] = useState('DAYMARK');
  const [selectedTopics, setSelectedTopics] = useState<NewsTopic[]>(['technology', 'world', 'business', 'science']);
  const [customInterests, setCustomInterests] = useState<string[]>(['Artificial Intelligence', 'Clean Energy']);
  const [interestInput, setInterestInput] = useState('');
  const [readingPace, setReadingPace] = useState<'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'>('standard');
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const user = getCurrentUser();
    if (!user) {
      window.location.href = '/login?redirect=/onboarding';
      return;
    }
    const loaded = getStoredPreferences();
    setPreferences(loaded);
    setReaderName(user?.name || loaded.readerName || 'Reader');
    setNewspaperTitle(user?.paperTitle || loaded.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK');
    setSelectedTopics(loaded.selectedTopics.length > 0 ? loaded.selectedTopics : ['technology', 'world', 'business', 'science']);
    setCustomInterests(loaded.customInterests.length > 0 ? loaded.customInterests : ['Artificial Intelligence', 'Clean Energy']);
    setReadingPace(loaded.readingPace || 'standard');
  }, []);

  const toggleTopic = (topic: NewsTopic) => {
    if (selectedTopics.includes(topic)) {
      if (selectedTopics.length > 1) {
        setSelectedTopics(selectedTopics.filter((t) => t !== topic));
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedTopics([...selectedTopics, topic]);
    }
  };

  const addCustomInterest = (interest: string) => {
    const trimmed = interest.trim();
    if (trimmed && !customInterests.some((i) => i.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) {
      setCustomInterests([...customInterests, trimmed]);
      setInterestInput('');
    }
  };

  const removeCustomInterest = (interestToRemove: string) => {
    setCustomInterests(customInterests.filter((i) => i !== interestToRemove));
  };

  const handleFinish = () => {
    setIsSaving(true);
    const updated: UserPreferences = {
      ...preferences,
      readerName: readerName.trim() || 'Reader',
      newspaperTitle: newspaperTitle.trim().toUpperCase() || 'DAYMARK',
      selectedTopics,
      customInterests,
      readingPace,
      onboarded: true,
    };

    saveStoredPreferences(updated);

    addLearningSignal({
      type: 'calibrate',
      articleTitle: `Editorial Calibration: ${selectedTopics.length} Focus Topics, ${customInterests.length} Lenses`,
      impactDescription: `Pace set to ${readingPace.toUpperCase()} (${readingPace === 'quick' ? '5m' : readingPace === 'standard' ? '15m' : '30m'}). Ready for daily dispatch.`,
    });

    router.push('/');
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 flex flex-col justify-between text-charcoal-900 font-sans selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Top Hairline */}
      <div className="h-1.5 bg-forest-700 w-full" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/70">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center space-x-2 group">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105" />
            <span className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
              DAYMARK
            </span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4 text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-600">
            <span>Step {step} of 3</span>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5">
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${step >= 1 ? 'bg-forest-600' : 'bg-ivory-300'}`} />
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${step >= 2 ? 'bg-forest-600' : 'bg-ivory-300'}`} />
              <span className={`w-2 h-2 rounded-full ${step >= 3 ? 'bg-forest-600' : 'bg-ivory-300'}`} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Container */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-4xl w-full mx-auto p-4 sm:p-8 flex items-center">
        <div className="w-full bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded shadow-xs p-6 sm:p-10">
          {/* STEP 1: TOPICS SELECTION */}
          {step === 1 && (
            <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
              <div className="border-b border-ivory-200 pb-4">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700">
                  Step 1 of 3 • Core Editorial Sections
                </span>
                <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
                  Select Your Editorial Desks
                </h1>
                <p className="font-serif italic text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
                  Choose the sections that form the backbone of your morning broadsheet.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
                {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((topic) => {
                  const isSelected = selectedTopics.includes(topic.id);
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={topic.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => toggleTopic(topic.id)}
                      className={`p-4 rounded border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-start justify-between ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'border-forest-600 bg-forest-50/60 ring-1 ring-forest-600/30'
                          : 'border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/50 hover:border-charcoal-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                          <span className={`text-sm font-sans font-bold ${isSelected ? 'text-forest-900' : 'text-charcoal-900'}`}>
                            {topic.label}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-600 font-serif mt-1 leading-snug">
                          {topic.description}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <div
                        className={`w-5 h-5 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ml-3 transition-colors ${
                          isSelected ? 'bg-forest-600 text-white' : 'border border-ivory-300 text-transparent'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <Check className="w-3 h-3 stroke-[3]" />
                      </div>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-6 border-t border-ivory-200">
                <span className="text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500">
                  {selectedTopics.length} sections selected (minimum 1 required)
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStep(2)}
                  className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <span>Continue to Custom Lenses</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 2: CUSTOM KEYWORDS */}
          {step === 2 && (
            <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
              <div className="border-b border-ivory-200 pb-4">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700">
                  Step 2 of 3 • Precision Ranking
                </span>
                <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
                  Tailor Your Intellectual Lenses
                </h1>
                <p className="font-serif italic text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
                  Articles matching these terms will be prioritized on your front page with up to +35 match score points.
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Input row */}
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={interestInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setInterestInput(e.target.value)}
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      addCustomInterest(interestInput);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder="e.g. Clean Energy, Large Language Models, Solid-State Batteries..."
                  className="flex-1 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => addCustomInterest(interestInput)}
                  className="px-4 py-2.5 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-black text-ivory-50 font-sans text-xs font-medium rounded flex items-center space-x-1 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
                  <span>Add Lens</span>
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Active Lenses */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  Active Prioritized Terms ({customInterests.length})
                </label>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 min-h-[44px] p-3 bg-ivory-50/70 border border-ivory-200 rounded">
                  {customInterests.length === 0 ? (
                    <span className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-400">
                      No custom lenses added yet. Click suggestions below or type your own.
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    customInterests.map((interest) => (
                      <span
                        key={interest}
                        className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-forest-100/70 text-forest-900 text-xs font-serif border border-forest-200"
                      >
                        <span>#{interest}</span>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => removeCustomInterest(interest)}
                          className="text-forest-700 hover:text-red-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                        </button>
                      </span>
                    ))
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Suggestions */}
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2">
                  1-Click Suggestions
                </label>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS.map((sug) => {
                    const isAdded = customInterests.some((i) => i.toLowerCase() === sug.toLowerCase());
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={sug}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => addCustomInterest(sug)}
                        disabled={isAdded}
                        className={`text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded border transition-all ${
                          isAdded
                            ? 'bg-ivory-200/50 text-charcoal-400 border-ivory-200 cursor-default'
                            : 'bg-white hover:bg-forest-50 text-charcoal-700 hover:text-forest-800 border-ivory-300 cursor-pointer'
                        }`}
                      >
                        +{sug}
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-6 border-t border-ivory-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStep(1)}
                  className="text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900"
                >
                  ← Back to Topics
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStep(3)}
                  className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <span>Continue to Reading Pace</span>
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* STEP 3: READING PACE & MASTHEAD */}
          {step === 3 && (
            <div className="space-y-6 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
              <div className="border-b border-ivory-200 pb-4">
                <span className="text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700">
                  Step 3 of 3 • Publication Details
                </span>
                <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
                  Set Your Daily Reading Pace
                </h1>
                <p className="font-serif italic text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
                  Tune article depth and length according to your morning routine.
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Reading Pace Options */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                {[
                  {
                    id: 'quick' as const,
                    title: 'Quick Scan',
                    time: '~5 Minutes',
                    desc: 'Tight executive summaries and breaking dispatches.',
                  },
                  {
                    id: 'standard' as const,
                    title: 'Standard Dispatch',
                    time: '~15 Minutes',
                    desc: 'Balanced broadsheet with cover story and reports.',
                  },
                  {
                    id: 'deep' as const,
                    title: 'Deep Broadside',
                    time: '~30 Minutes',
                    desc: 'In-depth essays, investigative reports, and analysis.',
                  },
                ].map((pace) => {
                  const isSelected = readingPace === pace.id;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={pace.id}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setReadingPace(pace.id)}
                      className={`p-4 rounded border text-left transition-all cursor-pointer ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'border-forest-600 bg-forest-50/70 ring-1 ring-forest-600/30'
                          : 'border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/50 hover:border-charcoal-400'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                        <span className="font-sans font-bold text-sm text-charcoal-900">
                          {pace.title}
                        </span>
                        <Clock className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isSelected ? 'text-forest-600' : 'text-charcoal-400'}`} />
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs font-serif italic text-forest-700 block mb-1">
                        {pace.time}
                      </span>
                      <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-600 font-serif leading-snug">
                        {pace.desc}
                      </p>
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>

              {/* Name & Title Inputs */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                    Subscriber Name
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={readerName}
                    onChange={(e) => setReaderName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Eleanor Vance"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 font-sans focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                    Newspaper Masthead Title
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={newspaperTitle}
                    onChange={(e) => setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. DAYMARK"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 font-sans uppercase font-bold tracking-wider focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-6 border-t border-ivory-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setStep(2)}
                  className="text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900"
                >
                  ← Back to Lenses
                </button>
                <button
                  id="btn-onboarding-finish"
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleFinish}
                  disabled={isSaving}
                  className="px-8 py-3 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-black text-ivory-50 font-sans text-xs font-bold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <span>{isSaving ? 'Compiling Paper...' : 'Generate My Morning Edition →'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-ivory-300 py-4 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
        <p>Daymark — Verified News Wire • 100% Free Zero-Budget Architecture • NaSCon &apos;26</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/profile/page.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import {
  Brain,
  Sparkles,
  ArrowLeft,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  ThumbsUp,
  ThumbsDown,
  X,
  Bookmark,
  Calendar,
  Activity,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  ArrowRight,
  TrendingUp,
  RefreshCw,
  Newspaper,
  Plus,
  Check,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AVAILABLE_TOPICS, NewsTopic, UserFeedback, UserPreferences, LearningSignal, SuggestedInterest } from '@/types/news';
import {
  getStoredPreferences,
  getStoredFeedback,
  getLearningSignals,
  getCurrentUser,
  saveStoredFeedback,
  saveStoredPreferences,
  addLearningSignal,
} from '@/lib/storage';

export default function ProfileLearningPage() {
  const [preferences, setPreferences] = useState<UserPreferences>(getStoredPreferences);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<UserFeedback>(getStoredFeedback);
  const [signals, setSignals] = useState<LearningSignal[]>([]);
  const [userTitle, setUserTitle] = useState('DAYMARK');
  const [userName, setUserName] = useState('Reader');
  const [suggestedInterests, setSuggestedInterests] = useState<SuggestedInterest[]>([]);
  const [feedbackMsg, setFeedbackMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const prefs = getStoredPreferences();
    const fb = getStoredFeedback();
    const sigs = getLearningSignals();
    const user = getCurrentUser();

    setPreferences(prefs);
    setFeedback(fb);
    setSignals(sigs);
    setUserTitle(user?.paperTitle || prefs.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK');
    setUserName(user?.name || prefs.readerName || 'Reader');

    // Fetch live AI suggested interests from server database
    fetch(`/api/ai/learn?userId=${user?.id || 'judge-nascon-2026'}`)
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        if (data?.suggestions) {
          setSuggestedInterests(data.suggestions);
        }
      })
      .catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const handleAcceptSuggestion = async (topic: string, category: NewsTopic | string = 'technology') => {
    const current = preferences.customInterests || [];
    if (!current.includes(topic)) {
      const updatedPrefs = { ...preferences, customInterests: [...current, topic] };
      setPreferences(updatedPrefs);
      saveStoredPreferences(updatedPrefs);
    }

    setSuggestedInterests((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.topic.toLowerCase() === topic.toLowerCase() ? { ...s, accepted: true } : s))
    );

    addLearningSignal({
      type: 'calibrate',
      articleTitle: `Adopted AI Topic: ${topic}`,
      topic: category,
      impactDescription: `Integrated AI-discovered interest "${topic}" into morning paper desks.`,
    });

    setFeedbackMsg(`Added "${topic}" to your morning paper desks!`);
    setTimeout(() => setFeedbackMsg(null), 3000);

    try {
      await fetch('/api/ai/learn', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: 'accept', topic }),
      });
    } catch {}
  };

  const handleDismissSuggestion = async (topic: string) => {
    setSuggestedInterests((prev) =>
      prev.map((s) => (s.topic.toLowerCase() === topic.toLowerCase() ? { ...s, dismissed: true } : s))
    );

    try {
      await fetch('/api/ai/learn', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ type: 'dismiss', topic }),
      });
    } catch {}
  };

  // Compute calculated topic weights (0 - 100%)
  const topicWeights = AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((topic) => {
    const isSelected = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(topic.id);
    const affinity = feedback.topicAffinities[topic.id] || 0; // -5 to +5
    // Base 50% for selected, 15% for unselected, +/- affinity * 8%
    const base = isSelected ? 60 : 20;
    const computed = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(base + affinity * 8), 5), 98);

    return {
      ...topic,
      isSelected,
      affinity,
      weightPct: computed,
    };
  }).sort((a, b) => b.weightPct - a.weightPct);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 flex flex-col justify-between text-charcoal-900 font-sans selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Top Hairline */}
      <div className="h-1.5 bg-forest-700 w-full" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/70">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-4">
            <Link href="/" className="flex items-center space-x-2 group">
              <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105" />
              <span className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
                {userTitle}
              </span>
            </Link>
            <span className="text-ivory-400">•</span>
            <span className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-600">
              The Learning Brain & Recommendation Profile
            </span>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-ivory-200/80 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-800 transition-colors font-medium"
            >
              <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Back to Today&apos;s Edition</span>
            </Link>
            <Link
              href="/onboarding"
              className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white transition-colors font-semibold"
            >
              <SlidersHorizontal className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Calibrate Lenses</span>
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Hero Strip */}
      <div className="bg-ivory-50 border-b border-ivory-200 py-8 px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-start md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700 mb-1">
              <Brain className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Algorithmic Transparency Engine</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Editorial Learning Brain for {userName}
            </h1>
            <p className="font-serif italic text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal-600 mt-1 max-w-2xl">
              Daymark uses a transparent, deterministic ranking model. Every thumbs-up, skip, and clipping directly adjusts tomorrow&apos;s broadsheet without black-box tracking.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-3">
            <div className="text-center px-3 border-r border-ivory-200">
              <span className="text-xl font-headline font-bold text-forest-800 block">
                {feedback.likedArticleIds?.length || 0}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Upvoted
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center px-3 border-r border-ivory-200">
              <span className="text-xl font-headline font-bold text-red-700 block">
                {feedback.skippedArticleIds?.length || 0}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Skipped
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center px-3">
              <span className="text-xl font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900 block">
                {feedback.savedArticleIds?.length || 0}
              </span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Clippings
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Dashboard Body */}
      <main className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-8 flex-1 w-full space-y-8">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-8">
          {/* LEFT COLUMN: DYNAMIC TOPIC WEIGHTS */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-6">
            <div className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-6 shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-ivory-200 pb-3 mb-5">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-headline text-xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 flex items-center space-x-2">
                    <Layers className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700" />
                    <span>Dynamic Topic Weights</span>
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-500 mt-0.5">
                    How likely each desk is to appear on your front page.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Link
                  href="/onboarding"
                  className="text-xs font-sans text-forest-700 hover:underline font-semibold"
                >
                  Adjust Sections →
                </Link>
              </div>

              {/* Progress Bars */}
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {topicWeights.map((topic) => (
                  <div key={topic.id} className="space-y-1.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
                      <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                        <span className="font-bold text-charcoal-900">
                          {topic.label}
                        </span>
                        {topic.isSelected && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.2 bg-forest-50 text-forest-700 rounded border border-forest-100 font-medium">
                            Core Desk
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {topic.affinity > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-forest-700 font-serif italic">
                            +{topic.affinity * 10}% boost
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {topic.affinity < 0 && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-red-600 font-serif italic">
                            {topic.affinity * 10}% penalty
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <span className="font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900">
                        {topic.weightPct}%
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* Progress Track */}
                    <div className="w-full bg-ivory-200 h-2 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className={`h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500 ${
                          topic.weightPct > 70
                            ? 'bg-forest-600'
                            : topic.weightPct > 40
                            ? 'bg-charcoal-700'
                            : 'bg-ivory-400'
                        }`}
                        style={{ width: `${topic.weightPct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* TOMORROW'S FORECAST BOX */}
            <div className="bg-forest-50/70 border border-forest-200 rounded p-6">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2 text-xs font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-900 mb-2">
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
                <span>Tomorrow&apos;s Broadsheet Forecast</span>
              </div>
              <p className="font-serif text-sm text-charcoal-800 leading-relaxed mb-3">
                Based on your {feedback.likedArticleIds?.length || 0} upvotes and {feedback.skippedArticleIds?.length || 0} skipped stories, tomorrow&apos;s morning wire will allocate prime front-page positioning to <span className="font-bold">{topicWeights[0]?.label}</span> and <span className="font-bold">{topicWeights[1]?.label}</span>, while reducing lifestyle and opinion dispatches.
              </p>
              <div className="flex items-center text-xs font-sans text-forest-800 font-medium space-x-1">
                <span>Next morning broadsheet ready at:</span>
                <span className="font-bold">6:00 AM Local Time</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* RIGHT COLUMN: ACTIVE LENSES & SIGNALS TIMELINE */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5 space-y-6">
            {/* REAL-TIME AI INFERRED INTERESTS */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-6 shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-ivory-200 pb-3 mb-4">
                <h3 className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                  <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
                  <span>Real-Time AI Inferred Desks</span>
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono bg-forest-100 text-forest-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded font-semibold">
                  Live Adaptive Matrix
                </span>
              </div>

              {feedbackMsg && (
                <div className="mb-4 p-2.5 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs rounded flex items-center gap-1.5 font-sans font-medium">
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-600 shrink-0" />
                  <span>{feedbackMsg}</span>
                </div>
              )}

              {suggestedInterests.filter((s) => !s.dismissed).length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-400 py-3 text-center">
                  All AI recommendations processed. Interact with more stories to trigger new discoveries!
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                  {suggestedInterests
                    .filter((s) => !s.dismissed)
                    .slice(0, 4)
                    .map((sug) => (
                      <div
                        key={sug.id}
                        className={`p-3 border rounded transition-colors ${
                          sug.accepted
                            ? 'bg-forest-50/60 border-forest-300'
                            : 'bg-ivory-50/70 border-ivory-200 hover:border-ivory-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] font-sans mb-1">
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-forest-700 bg-white px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-ivory-200">
                            {sug.confidence}% Match
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-charcoal-500 font-serif italic">{sug.category}</span>
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="font-headline text-sm font-bold text-charcoal-900 mb-1">
                          {sug.topic}
                        </h4>
                        <p className="font-serif text-xs text-charcoal-600 leading-relaxed mb-2.5">
                          {sug.reason}
                        </p>

                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-ivory-200 text-xs font-sans">
                          {sug.accepted ? (
                            <span className="text-forest-700 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px]">
                              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                              Active in Your Morning Paper
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => handleAcceptSuggestion(sug.topic, sug.category)}
                                className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white rounded font-semibold text-[11px] flex items-center gap-1 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                              >
                                <Plus className="w-3 h-3" />
                                Add to Daily Paper
                              </button>
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => handleDismissSuggestion(sug.topic)}
                                className="text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-700 text-[11px] transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                              >
                                Dismiss
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Active Keywords */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-6 shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-ivory-200 pb-3 mb-4">
                <h3 className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                  <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
                  <span>Prioritized Keyword Lenses</span>
                </h3>
                <Link
                  href="/onboarding"
                  className="text-xs font-sans text-forest-700 hover:underline font-semibold"
                >
                  Edit
                </Link>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {preferences.customInterests.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-400">
                    No custom keywords active.
                  </p>
                ) : (
                  preferences.customInterests.map((interest) => (
                    <span
                      key={interest}
                      className="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 text-charcoal-800 text-xs font-serif italic"
                    >
                      #{interest}
                    </span>
                  ))
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* REAL-TIME LEARNING SIGNALS LOG */}
            <div className="bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded p-6 shadow-2xs">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-ivory-200 pb-3 mb-4">
                <h3 className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900 flex items-center space-x-1.5">
                  <Activity className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
                  <span>Recent Learning Signals</span>
                </h3>
                <span className="text-[10px] font-sans uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-400 font-medium">
                  Deterministic Log
                </span>
              </div>

              {signals.length === 0 ? (
                <p className="text-xs font-serif italic text-charcoal-400 py-4 text-center">
                  No interaction signals captured yet. Like or skip articles on the front page to generate signals!
                </p>
              ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3.5 max-h-[380px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {signals.slice(0, 10).map((sig) => {
                    const date = new Date(sig.timestamp);
                    const timeStr = date.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });

                    return (
                      <div
                        key={sig.id}
                        className="p-3 bg-ivory-50/80 border border-ivory-200 rounded text-xs space-y-1"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans">
                          <span
                            className={`font-bold uppercase tracking-wider ${
                              sig.type === 'upvote'
                                ? 'text-forest-700'
                                : sig.type === 'skip' || sig.type === 'downvote'
                                ? 'text-red-700'
                                : sig.type === 'save'
                                ? 'text-charcoal-900'
                                : 'text-blue-700'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {sig.type === 'upvote' && '▲ Upvoted'}
                            {sig.type === 'skip' && '✕ Skipped Story'}
                            {sig.type === 'downvote' && '▼ Downvoted'}
                            {sig.type === 'save' && '🔖 Saved Clipping'}
                            {sig.type === 'calibrate' && '⚙️ Calibrated'}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-charcoal-400 font-serif italic">{timeStr}</span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="font-headline font-semibold text-charcoal-900 text-xs line-clamp-1">
                          {sig.articleTitle}
                        </p>
                        <p className="font-serif text-[11px] text-charcoal-600 leading-snug">
                          {sig.impactDescription}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-ivory-300 py-4 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
        <p>Daymark — Zero Black-Box Tracking • 100% Deterministic Curation • NaSCon &apos;26</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/signup/page.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Newspaper, UserPlus, ArrowRight, Sparkles, AlertCircle, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import {
  getStoredUsers,
  saveStoredUser,
  setCurrentUser,
  getStoredPreferences,
  saveStoredPreferences,
  addLearningSignal,
} from '@/lib/storage';
import { UserAccount } from '@/types/news';

export default function SignupPage() {
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [paperTitle, setPaperTitle] = useState('THE MORNING CHRONICLE');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [redirectParam, setRedirectParam] = useState<string | null>(null);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const redir = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('redirect');
      if (redir) setRedirectParam(redir);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleQuickFill = () => {
    setFullName('Sarah Lin');
    setEmail('sarah.lin@example.com');
    setPassword('subscriber123');
    setPaperTitle('THE LIN DISPATCH');
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setError(null);

    const cleanName = fullName.trim() || 'Subscriber';
    const cleanEmail = email.trim().toLowerCase();
    const cleanTitle = paperTitle.trim() || 'DAYMARK';

    if (!cleanEmail || !cleanEmail.includes('@')) {
      setError('Please enter a valid subscriber email address.');
      return;
    }
    if (password.length < 4) {
      setError('Password must be at least 4 characters.');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);

    const newUser: UserAccount = {
      id: `user-${Date.now()}`,
      name: cleanName,
      email: cleanEmail,
      password,
      paperTitle: cleanTitle.toUpperCase(),
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    // Save user and set active session
    saveStoredUser(newUser);
    setCurrentUser(newUser);

    // Sync preferences for new subscriber
    const currentPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
    saveStoredPreferences({
      ...currentPrefs,
      readerName: cleanName,
      newspaperTitle: cleanTitle.toUpperCase(),
      onboarded: false,
    });

    addLearningSignal({
      type: 'calibrate',
      articleTitle: `Subscription Initialized: ${cleanTitle.toUpperCase()}`,
      impactDescription: `New broadsheet created for ${cleanName}. Proceeding to desk calibration.`,
    });

    // Set authentication cookies for Next.js Middleware validation
    document.cookie = `daymark_token=${btoa(JSON.stringify({ id: newUser.id, email: newUser.email, name: newUser.name }))}; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;
    document.cookie = `daymark_otp_verified=true; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;

    setSuccessMsg(`Account created for ${cleanName}! Loading calibration desk...`);
    setTimeout(() => {
      window.location.href = '/onboarding';
    }, 400);
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-ivory-100 flex flex-col justify-between text-charcoal-900 font-sans selection:bg-forest-100 selection:text-forest-900">
      {/* Top Hairline */}
      <div className="h-1.5 bg-forest-700 w-full" />

      {/* Header */}
      <header className="border-b border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50/70">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link href="/" className="flex items-center space-x-2 group">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700 transition-transform group-hover:scale-105" />
            <span className="font-headline text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
              DAYMARK
            </span>
          </Link>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 text-xs font-sans">
            <Link
              href="/"
              className="text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900 font-medium"
            >
              ← Back to Paper
            </Link>
            <span className="text-ivory-400">|</span>
            <Link
              href={`/login${redirectParam ? `?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirectParam)}` : ''}`}
              className="font-semibold text-forest-700 hover:text-forest-900"
            >
              Already Subscribed? Sign In →
            </Link>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Container */}
      <main className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center p-4 sm:p-8">
        <div className="w-full max-w-lg bg-white border border-ivory-300 rounded shadow-xs p-6 sm:p-8 relative">
          {/* Header */}
          <div className="text-center mb-6 border-b border-ivory-200 pb-5">
            <div className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-forest-700 mb-1.5">
              <UserPlus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Subscription Desk</span>
            </div>
            <h1 className="font-headline text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Subscribe to Your Morning Paper
            </h1>
            <p className="font-serif italic text-xs sm:text-sm text-charcoal-600 mt-1 max-w-md mx-auto">
              Curate real, verified news delivered daily in an elegant, quiet broadsheet.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Quick Demo Fill button */}
          <div className="mb-5 flex justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleQuickFill}
              className="text-xs text-forest-700 hover:text-forest-900 underline font-medium flex items-center space-x-1"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-3 h-3" />
              <span>Auto-fill Sample Subscriber Data</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Alerts */}
          {error && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs text-red-700 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div className="mb-4 p-3 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded text-xs text-green-800 flex items-start space-x-2">
              <Check className="w-4 h-4 text-green-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
              <span>{successMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Form */}
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                Your Full Name
              </label>
              <input
                id="signup-name"
                type="text"
                required
                value={fullName}
                onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. Sarah Lin"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                Subscriber Email Address
              </label>
              <input
                id="signup-email"
                type="email"
                required
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="reader@example.com"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="signup-password"
                type="password"
                required
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Create a password"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                <label className="block text-xs font-sans font-semibold text-charcoal-700 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  Custom Newspaper Title
                </label>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-forest-700 font-serif italic">
                  Shown in your masthead
                </span>
              </div>
              <input
                id="signup-papertitle"
                type="text"
                value={paperTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setPaperTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. THE LIN DISPATCH"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-hidden focus:border-forest-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-sans uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold"
              />
            </div>

            <button
              id="btn-signup-submit"
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 bg-forest-700 hover:bg-forest-800 text-white font-sans text-xs font-semibold rounded tracking-wide transition-all shadow-xs flex items-center justify-center space-x-2 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 mt-4"
            >
              <span>{isLoading ? 'Setting Up Your Press...' : 'Subscribe & Calibrate Focus Desks →'}</span>
            </button>
          </form>

          {/* Footer note */}
          <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-ivory-200 text-center">
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 font-serif">
              Already have an account?{' '}
              <Link href={`/login${redirectParam ? `?redirect=${encodeURIComponent(redirectParam)}` : ''}`} className="text-forest-700 font-sans font-semibold hover:underline">
                Sign in to your press room
              </Link>
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-ivory-300 py-4 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500">
        <p>Daymark — Verified News Wire • 100% Client-Side LocalStorage Auth • NaSCon &apos;26</p>
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/verify-otp/page.tsx

'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Newspaper, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight, RotateCw, Sparkles, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { getCurrentUser, setCurrentUser, saveStoredUser, saveStoredPreferences, getStoredPreferences, addLearningSignal, DEMO_JUDGE_USER } from '@/lib/storage';

export default function VerifyOtpPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [otpDigits, setOtpDigits] = useState(['', '', '', '', '', '']);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMsg, setSuccessMsg] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isResending, setIsResending] = useState(false);
  const [redirectPath, setRedirectPath] = useState('/');
  const [dispatchedCode, setDispatchedCode] = useState<string | null>('742891');

  const inputRefs = useRef<(HTMLInputElement | null)[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Read query params from URL
    if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
      const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
      const urlEmail = params.get('email');
      const redir = params.get('redirect');
      if (urlEmail) {
        setEmail(urlEmail);
      } else {
        const current = getCurrentUser();
        setEmail(current?.email || DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email);
      }
      if (redir) setRedirectPath(redir);
    }
  }, []);

  const handleDigitChange = (index: number, val: string) => {
    if (!/^\d*$/.test(val)) return;

    const newDigits = [...otpDigits];
    newDigits[index] = val.slice(-1);
    setOtpDigits(newDigits);

    // Auto advance focus
    if (val && index < 5) {
      inputRefs.current[index + 1]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handleKeyDown = (index: number, e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !otpDigits[index] && index > 0) {
      inputRefs.current[index - 1]?.focus();
    }
  };

  const handlePaste = (e: React.ClipboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const pasted = e.clipboardData.getData('text').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
    if (!pasted) return;

    const newDigits = [...otpDigits];
    for (let i = 0; i < pasted.length; i++) {
      newDigits[i] = pasted[i];
    }
    setOtpDigits(newDigits);
    const focusIndex = Math.min(pasted.length, 5);
    inputRefs.current[focusIndex]?.focus();
  };

  const handleResend = async () => {
    if (!email) {
      setError('Please provide your subscriber email address.');
      return;
    }
    setIsResending(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/otp/send', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (data.success) {
        setSuccessMsg(data.message || 'Fresh OTP code dispatched!');
        if (data.demoOtpCode) {
          setDispatchedCode(data.demoOtpCode);
        }
      } else {
        setError(data.message || 'Failed to dispatch OTP.');
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Network error while requesting code.');
    } finally {
      setIsResending(false);
    }
  };

  const handleQuickFillJudge = () => {
    setEmail(DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email);
    setOtpDigits(['7', '4', '2', '8', '9', '1']);
    setDispatchedCode('742891');
    setError(null);
  };

  const handleVerify = async (e?: React.FormEvent) => {
    if (e) e.preventDefault();
    const fullCode = otpDigits.join('');
    if (fullCode.length < 6) {
      setError('Please enter all 6 digits of your verification code.');
      return;
    }

    setIsLoading(true);
    setError(null);

    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/auth/otp/verify', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email, code: fullCode }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (data.success && data.user) {
        // Sync with local client storage
        saveStoredUser(data.user);
        setCurrentUser(data.user);

        const currentPrefs = getStoredPreferences();
        saveStoredPreferences({
          ...currentPrefs,
          readerName: data.user.name,
          newspaperTitle: data.user.paperTitle,
          onboarded: true,
        });

        // Set client cookie for immediate Next.js hydration
        document.cookie = `daymark_otp_verified=true; path=/; max-age=604800; SameSite=Lax`;

        addLearningSignal({
          type: 'calibrate',
          articleTitle: 'OTP Identity Verified',
          impactDescription: `Verified subscriber session for ${data.user.email} with cryptographic broadsheet token.`,
        });

        setSuccessMsg('Press credentials verified! Redirecting to Today\'s Paper...');
        setTimeout(() => {
          window.location.href = redirectPath;
        }, 400);
      } else {
        setError(data.message || 'Verification failed. Please check the code.');
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('Connection error verifying OTP. Please try again.');
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#F7F5EF] text-[#20211F] flex flex-col justify-between selection:bg-[#496653] selection:text-white">
      {/* Top Broadsheet Masthead Banner */}
      <header className="border-b border-[#20211F]/20 py-5 bg-[#F7F5EF]">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 flex items-center justify-between">
          <Link href="/" className="group flex items-center gap-3">
            <Newspaper className="w-6 h-6 text-[#496653] group-hover:rotate-[-6deg] transition-transform" />
            <span className="font-serif text-2xl tracking-widest font-black uppercase text-[#20211F]">
              Daymark
            </span>
          </Link>
          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-xs uppercase tracking-widest font-mono text-[#20211F]/70">
              Security Desk • OTP Verification
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Main Verification Card */}
      <main className="flex-1 max-w-lg w-full mx-auto px-4 py-12 flex flex-col justify-center">
        <div className="bg-white border border-[#20211F]/20 shadow-[0_4px_24px_rgba(32,33,31,0.06)] p-8 sm:p-10 relative">
          {/* Broadsheet Corner Stamp */}
          <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 border-l border-b border-[#20211F]/20 bg-[#F7F5EF] px-3 py-1 font-mono text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-[#496653] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
            <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            Verified Dispatch
          </div>

          <div className="text-center mb-6">
            <h1 className="font-serif text-3xl font-black tracking-tight text-[#20211F] uppercase mb-2">
              Subscriber Verification
            </h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#20211F]/75 font-serif leading-relaxed">
              Enter the 6-digit Press Verification OTP code dispatched to confirm your identity.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Quick-fill Demo Box for Hackathon Judges */}
          <div className="mb-6 p-3.5 bg-[#496653]/10 border border-[#496653]/30 rounded-none text-left">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[#496653] uppercase tracking-wider">
                <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
                Hackathon Judge Quick-Fill
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleQuickFillJudge}
                className="text-xs font-mono font-bold bg-[#496653] text-white px-2.5 py-1 hover:bg-[#3d5545] transition-colors"
              >
                1-Click Fill (742891)
              </button>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#20211F]/75 mt-1 font-serif">
              Pre-authorized demo code: <strong className="font-mono text-[#496653]">742891</strong> for{' '}
              <span className="font-mono">{email || 'judge@nascon2026.org'}</span>.
            </p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-red-600" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          {successMsg && (
            <div className="mb-6 p-3 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs flex items-center gap-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
              <span>{successMsg}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <form onSubmit={handleVerify} className="space-y-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs uppercase font-mono tracking-wider text-[#20211F]/70 mb-1">
                Subscriber Email
              </label>
              <input
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="subscriber@example.org"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-[#F7F5EF]/60 border border-[#20211F]/30 focus:border-[#496653] focus:outline-none font-mono"
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs uppercase font-mono tracking-wider text-[#20211F]/70 mb-3 text-center">
                6-Digit Press OTP Code
              </label>
              <div className="flex justify-between gap-2 sm:gap-3">
                {otpDigits.map((digit, idx) => (
                  <input
                    key={idx}
                    ref={(el) => {
                      inputRefs.current[idx] = el;
                    }}
                    type="text"
                    inputMode="numeric"
                    maxLength={1}
                    value={digit}
                    onChange={(e) => handleDigitChange(idx, e.target.value)}
                    onKeyDown={(e) => handleKeyDown(idx, e)}
                    onPaste={handlePaste}
                    className="w-12 h-14 text-center font-mono text-2xl font-black bg-[#F7F5EF] border-2 border-[#20211F]/30 focus:border-[#496653] focus:bg-white focus:outline-none transition-all"
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isLoading || otpDigits.join('').length < 6}
              className="w-full py-3.5 bg-[#20211F] text-white hover:bg-[#496653] disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 uppercase tracking-widest text-xs font-bold"
            >
              {isLoading ? (
                <>
                  <RotateCw className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
                  Verifying Press Credentials...
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  Confirm & Enter Broadsheet
                  <ArrowRight className="w-4 h-4" />
                </>
              )}
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-[#20211F]/10 text-xs">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleResend}
                disabled={isResending}
                className="font-mono text-[#496653] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                <RotateCw className={`w-3.5 h-3.5 ${isResending ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                {isResending ? 'Dispatching...' : 'Resend Code'}
              </button>
              <Link href="/login" className="font-serif text-[#20211F]/60 hover:text-[#20211F]">
                Back to Sign In
              </Link>
            </div>
          </form>
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Broadsheet Footer */}
      <footer className="border-t border-[#20211F]/20 py-4 text-center text-xs font-serif text-[#20211F]/60">
        Daymark Editorial Dispatch System • Cryptographic Press Verification v1.0
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/app/page.tsx
