FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/components/OnboardingModal.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Plus, Sparkles, Tag, Compass } from 'lucide-react';
import { UserPreferences, NewsTopic, AVAILABLE_TOPICS } from '@/types/news';

interface OnboardingModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
  onSave: (newPrefs: UserPreferences) => void;
  isFirstVisit?: boolean;
}

const INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS = [
  'Artificial Intelligence',
  'Space Exploration',
  'Clean Energy',
  'Quantum Computing',
  'Global Geopolitics',
  'Semiconductors & Chips',
  'Biotechnology',
  'Electric Vehicles',
  'Venture Capital',
  'Cybersecurity',
  'Macroeconomics',
  'Neuroscience',
];

export default function OnboardingModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  preferences,
  onSave,
  isFirstVisit = false,
}: OnboardingModalProps) {
  const [readerName, setReaderName] = useState(preferences.readerName || '');
  const [newspaperTitle, setNewspaperTitle] = useState(preferences.newspaperTitle || 'DAYMARK');
  const [selectedTopics, setSelectedTopics] = useState<NewsTopic[]>(preferences.selectedTopics || ['technology', 'world']);
  const [customInterests, setCustomInterests] = useState<string[]>(preferences.customInterests || []);
  const [interestInput, setInterestInput] = useState('');
  const [readingPace, setReadingPace] = useState<'quick' | 'standard' | 'deep'>(preferences.readingPace || 'standard');

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const toggleTopic = (topic: NewsTopic) => {
    if (selectedTopics.includes(topic)) {
      if (selectedTopics.length > 1) {
        setSelectedTopics(selectedTopics.filter((t) => t !== topic));
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedTopics([...selectedTopics, topic]);
    }
  };

  const addCustomInterest = (interest: string) => {
    const trimmed = interest.trim();
    if (trimmed && !customInterests.some((i) => i.toLowerCase() === trimmed.toLowerCase())) {
      setCustomInterests([...customInterests, trimmed]);
      setInterestInput('');
    }
  };

  const removeCustomInterest = (interestToRemove: string) => {
    setCustomInterests(customInterests.filter((i) => i !== interestToRemove));
  };

  const handleKeyDownInterest = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
      e.preventDefault();
      addCustomInterest(interestInput);
    }
  };

  const handleSave = () => {
    onSave({
      ...preferences,
      readerName: readerName.trim() || 'Reader',
      newspaperTitle: newspaperTitle.trim() || 'DAYMARK',
      selectedTopics,
      customInterests,
      readingPace,
      onboarded: true,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div 
        className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-2xl w-full max-h-[92vh] overflow-y-auto p-6 sm:p-8"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="onboarding-title"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-4 border-b border-ivory-300">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-forest-700 font-sans font-semibold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest">
              <Compass className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Welcome to Daymark' : 'Curate Your Morning Broadsheet'}</span>
            </div>
            <h2 id="onboarding-title" className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
              Personalize Your Edition
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm font-serif italic text-charcoal-600 mt-1">
              Your world, clearly delivered. Select your focus areas and topics for a tailored daily dispatch.
            </p>
          </div>
          {!isFirstVisit && (
            <button
              onClick={onClose}
              className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
              aria-label="Close preferences"
            >
              <X className="w-5 h-5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-6 py-4">
          {/* Section 1: Masthead Identity */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
                Your Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={readerName}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setReaderName(e.target.value);
                  if (newspaperTitle === 'DAYMARK' || newspaperTitle.includes('DAYMARK')) {
                    setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value.trim() ? `${e.target.value.trim().toUpperCase()} DAYMARK` : 'DAYMARK');
                  }
                }}
                placeholder="e.g. Eleanor Vance"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-serif"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
                Publication Masthead Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={newspaperTitle}
                onChange={(e) => setNewspaperTitle(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. DAYMARK"
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-headline uppercase"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 2: Core News Topics */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-2">
              Select Editorial Topics (At least 1)
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-2.5">
              {AVAILABLE_TOPICS.map((topic) => {
                const isSelected = selectedTopics.includes(topic.id);
                return (
                  <button
                    key={topic.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleTopic(topic.id)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded border text-left flex flex-col justify-between transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 text-charcoal-900 shadow-2xs'
                        : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-200 text-charcoal-600 hover:border-ivory-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between w-full mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{topic.label}</span>
                      {isSelected ? (
                        <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-700" />
                      ) : (
                        <div className="w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border border-ivory-400" />
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-charcoal-500 line-clamp-1">{topic.description}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 3: Custom Interests & Keyword Boosters */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-1">
              Custom Topic Lenses & Priority Keywords
            </label>
            <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 mb-2">
              Add niche interests. Our ranking engine elevates stories matching these exact phrases and subjects.
            </p>

            {/* Input row */}
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2.5">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={interestInput}
                onChange={(e) => setInterestInput(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={handleKeyDownInterest}
                placeholder="e.g. Clean Energy, SpaceX, Venture Capital..."
                className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 bg-ivory-50 border border-ivory-300 rounded text-sm text-charcoal-900 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-forest-600 font-serif"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => addCustomInterest(interestInput)}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium hover:bg-black transition-colors flex items-center space-x-1"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                <span>Add</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Active custom tags */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 min-h-[34px] p-2 bg-ivory-200/50 rounded border border-ivory-300 mb-2">
              {customInterests.length === 0 ? (
                <span className="text-xs text-charcoal-400 font-serif italic">No custom lenses added yet. Click suggestions below or type your own.</span>
              ) : (
                customInterests.map((interest) => (
                  <span
                    key={interest}
                    className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 px-2.5 py-1 bg-ivory-50 border border-forest-300 rounded-full text-xs font-sans font-medium text-forest-800 shadow-2xs"
                  >
                    <span>{interest}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => removeCustomInterest(interest)}
                      className="text-charcoal-400 hover:text-terracotta-600 ml-1"
                    >
                      <X className="w-3 h-3" />
                    </button>
                  </span>
                ))
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Quick Suggestions Chips */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mr-1 flex items-center">
                <Tag className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" /> Suggestions:
              </span>
              {INTEREST_SUGGESTIONS.filter((s) => !customInterests.includes(s)).slice(0, 6).map((s) => (
                <button
                  key={s}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => addCustomInterest(s)}
                  className="text-[11px] font-sans px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-ivory-200 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-700 rounded transition-colors"
                >
                  + {s}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Section 4: Reading Pace */}
          <div>
            <label className="block text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-charcoal-700 mb-2">
              Morning Reading Pace
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
              {[
                {
                  id: 'quick',
                  label: 'Quick Scan',
                  time: '~5 Minutes',
                  desc: 'Focuses on rapid wire briefs & top bullet points',
                },
                {
                  id: 'standard',
                  label: 'Standard Edition',
                  time: '~15 Minutes',
                  desc: 'A balanced broadsheet with lead stories and analysis',
                },
                {
                  id: 'deep',
                  label: 'Deep Dive',
                  time: '~30 Minutes',
                  desc: 'In-depth reporting, long reads, and commentary',
                },
              ].map((pace) => {
                const isSelected = readingPace === pace.id;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={pace.id}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setReadingPace(pace.id as any)}
                    className={`p-3 rounded border text-left transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 text-charcoal-900 shadow-2xs'
                        : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-200 text-charcoal-600 hover:border-ivory-300'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                      <span className="text-xs font-sans font-bold">{pace.label}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-forest-700 font-semibold">{pace.time}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 font-serif leading-tight">{pace.desc}</p>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Footer actions */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-500 italic">
            Deterministic rule-based ranking • Saved locally
          </span>
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-3">
            {!isFirstVisit && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onClose}
                className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-sans font-medium text-charcoal-600 hover:text-charcoal-900"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            )}
            <button
              id="btn-save-preferences-confirm"
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              className="px-6 py-2.5 bg-forest-600 hover:bg-forest-700 text-white rounded text-sm font-sans font-semibold tracking-wide shadow-xs transition-colors flex items-center space-x-2"
            >
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4 text-ivory-200" />
              <span>{isFirstVisit ? 'Generate My Morning Edition' : 'Update & Re-Rank Daymark'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { X, Calendar, Newspaper, Trash2, ArrowRight, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { SavedEdition } from '@/types/news';

interface SavedEditionsDrawerProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  editions: SavedEdition[];
  onSelectEdition: (edition: SavedEdition) => void;
  onDeleteEdition: (id: string) => void;
  onSaveCurrent: () => void;
  activeEditionId?: string | null;
  onResetToLive: () => void;
}

export default function SavedEditionsDrawer({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  editions,
  onSelectEdition,
  onDeleteEdition,
  onSaveCurrent,
  activeEditionId,
  onResetToLive,
}: SavedEditionsDrawerProps) {
  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 overflow-hidden bg-charcoal-900/50 backdrop-blur-xs flex justify-end animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="w-full max-w-md bg-ivory-100 h-full shadow-2xl border-l border-ivory-300 flex flex-col"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="archive-drawer-title"
      >
        {/* Drawer Header */}
        <div className="p-5 border-b border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between bg-ivory-50">
          <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
            <Newspaper className="w-5 h-5 text-forest-700" />
            <h3 id="archive-drawer-title" className="font-headline text-lg font-bold text-charcoal-900">
              Daymark Editions Archive
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close archive"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Current status bar */}
        <div className="px-5 py-3 bg-ivory-200/60 border-b border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between text-xs font-sans">
          {activeEditionId ? (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-forest-600 animate-pulse" />
              <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Archived Edition Loaded</span>
              <button
                onClick={onResetToLive}
                className="text-forest-700 underline ml-1 hover:text-forest-900 font-medium"
              >
                Return to Live Wire
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-forest-600" />
              <span className="font-medium text-charcoal-700">Currently viewing Live Edition</span>
            </div>
          )}
          <button
            onClick={onSaveCurrent}
            className="px-2.5 py-1 bg-charcoal-900 hover:bg-black text-ivory-50 rounded text-[11px] font-medium transition-colors"
          >
            + Save Issue
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Editions List */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-3">
          {editions.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-12 text-charcoal-500 font-serif">
              <BookOpen className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-ivory-400 mb-2" />
              <p className="font-bold text-charcoal-800">No saved editions yet</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-charcoal-500 mt-1 max-w-xs mx-auto">
                Snapshot today&apos;s morning paper to revisit headlines, articles, and your reading list later.
              </p>
              <button
                onClick={onSaveCurrent}
                className="mt-4 px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium"
              >
                Save Today&apos;s Edition
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            editions.map((ed) => {
              const isSelected = activeEditionId === ed.id;
              return (
                <div
                  key={ed.id}
                  className={`p-3.5 rounded border transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-forest-50 border-forest-600 shadow-2xs'
                      : 'bg-ivory-50/80 border-ivory-300 hover:border-ivory-400'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans text-charcoal-500 mb-1">
                    <span className="flex items-center font-medium text-forest-700">
                      <Calendar className="w-3 h-3 mr-1" />
                      {ed.dateStr}
                    </span>
                    <span>{ed.articlesCount} stories</span>
                  </div>

                  <h4 className="font-serif font-bold text-sm text-charcoal-900 line-clamp-2 leading-snug mb-1">
                    {ed.leadHeadline || 'Morning Edition Headlines'}
                  </h4>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 border-t border-ivory-200 text-xs font-sans mt-2">
                    <button
                      onClick={() => {
                        onSelectEdition(ed);
                        onClose();
                      }}
                      className="inline-flex items-center space-x-1 text-forest-700 font-semibold hover:underline"
                    >
                      <span>{isSelected ? 'Currently Viewing' : 'Read Edition'}</span>
                      <ArrowRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() => onDeleteEdition(ed.id)}
                      className="text-charcoal-400 hover:text-terracotta-600 p-1 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete saved edition"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="p-4 border-t border-ivory-300 bg-ivory-50 text-center text-xs font-serif text-charcoal-500">
          Saved offline to browser storage
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/components/SectionBlock.tsx

'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import ArticleCard from './ArticleCard';

interface SectionBlockProps {
  title: string;
  sectionCode?: string;
  articles: ScoredArticle[];
  onLike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onDislike: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onSkip: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onBookmark: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  onWhyThis: (article: ScoredArticle) => void;
  likedIds: string[];
  dislikedIds: string[];
  bookmarkedIds: string[];
}

export default function SectionBlock({
  title,
  sectionCode = 'SECTION',
  articles,
  onLike,
  onDislike,
  onSkip,
  onBookmark,
  onWhyThis,
  likedIds,
  dislikedIds,
  bookmarkedIds,
}: SectionBlockProps) {
  if (articles.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <section className="my-10 print-break-inside-avoid">
      {/* Section Header Rule */}
      <div className="flex items-center space-x-3 mb-5">
        <span className="font-sans font-bold text-[10px] uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-forest-700 bg-forest-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded border border-forest-100">
          {sectionCode}
        </span>
        <h3 className="font-headline text-xl sm:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-charcoal-900">
          {title}
        </h3>
        <div className="flex-1 h-px bg-ivory-300"></div>
      </div>

      {/* Grid of Section Articles */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {articles.map((article) => (
          <ArticleCard
            key={article.id}
            article={article}
            variant="standard"
            onLike={onLike}
            onDislike={onDislike}
            onSkip={onSkip}
            onBookmark={onBookmark}
            onWhyThis={onWhyThis}
            isLiked={likedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isDisliked={dislikedIds.includes(article.id)}
            isBookmarked={bookmarkedIds.includes(article.id)}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import React from 'react';

export function LoadingNewspaper() {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 py-16">

interface EmptyStateProps {
  onResetFilters: () => void;
  onOpenPreferences: () => void;
}

export function EmptyState({ onResetFilters, onOpenPreferences }: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto my-16 p-8 text-center bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-xs">
      <FilterX className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-charcoal-400 mb-3" />
      <h3 className="font-headline text-xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mb-2">
        No Stories In This Section
      </h3>
      <p className="font-serif italic text-xs text-charcoal-600 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        All stories for this section may have been read or dismissed, or your custom interest lenses may be overly restrictive.
      </p>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center space-x-3">
        <button
          onClick={onResetFilters}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-ivory-200 hover:bg-ivory-300 text-charcoal-800 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium transition-colors"
        >
          View All Topics
        </button>
        <button
          onClick={onOpenPreferences}
          className="px-4 py-2 bg-forest-600 hover:bg-forest-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium transition-colors"
        >
          Adjust Lenses
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface ErrorStateProps {
  error: string;
  onRetry: () => void;
}

export function ErrorState({ error, onRetry }: ErrorStateProps) {
  return (
    <div className="max-w-md mx-auto my-16 p-8 text-center bg-ivory-100 border border-terracotta-500/30 rounded-lg shadow-xs">
      <AlertTriangle className="w-10 h-10 mx-auto text-terracotta-600 mb-3" />
      <h3 className="font-headline text-xl font-bold text-charcoal-900 mb-2">
        Live Wire Interruption
      </h3>
      <p className="font-serif italic text-xs text-charcoal-700 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
        {error || 'Unable to retrieve live public feeds at this moment. The wire connection timed out or is unreachable.'}
      </p>
      <button
        onClick={onRetry}
        className="px-5 py-2.5 bg-forest-600 hover:bg-forest-700 text-white rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium transition-colors inline-flex items-center space-x-2"
      >
        <RotateCw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
        <span>Reconnect Wire Service</span>
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/components/WhyThisModal.tsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Sparkles, CheckCircle2, Clock, ThumbsUp, Compass, Target, BrainCircuit } from 'lucide-react';
import { ScoredArticle } from '@/lib/ranking';
import { UserPreferences } from '@/types/news';
import { fetchGeminiExplanation, getGeminiApiKey } from '@/lib/gemini';

interface WhyThisModalProps {
  article: ScoredArticle | null;
  onClose: () => void;
  preferences: UserPreferences;
}

export default function WhyThisModal({ article, onClose, preferences }: WhyThisModalProps) {
  const [aiAnalysis, setAiAnalysis] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isAiLoading, setIsAiLoading] = useState(false);
  const [isAiGenerated, setIsAiGenerated] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (article) {
      setAiAnalysis(null);
      setIsAiLoading(true);

      fetchGeminiExplanation(article, preferences)
        .then((res) => {
          setAiAnalysis(res.text);
          setIsAiGenerated(res.isAiGenerated);
        })
        .finally(() => {
          setIsAiLoading(false);
        });
    }
  }, [article, preferences]);

  if (!article) return null;

  const breakdown = article.scoreBreakdown || {
    topicScore: 20,
    keywordScore: 16,
    recencyScore: 16,
    paceScore: 10,
    feedbackScore: 0,
    total: article.relevanceScore || 85,
  };

  const hasKey = !!getGeminiApiKey();

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-charcoal-900/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150">
      <div 
        className="bg-ivory-100 border border-ivory-300 rounded-lg shadow-2xl max-w-lg w-full p-6 sm:p-7 max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto"
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="why-this-title"
      >
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between pb-3 border-b border-ivory-300">
          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-1.5 text-forest-700 font-sans font-semibold text-[11px] uppercase tracking-widest">
              <Sparkles className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Transparent Curation Logic</span>
            </div>
            <h3 id="why-this-title" className="text-xl font-headline font-bold text-charcoal-900 mt-1">
              Why this story was ranked for you
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onClose}
            className="p-1 text-charcoal-400 hover:text-charcoal-800 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Close dialog"
          >
            <X className="w-5 h-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Article Summary */}
        <div className="py-3 border-b border-ivory-200">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-700">
            {article.source.name} • {article.category.toUpperCase()}
          </span>
          <h4 className="text-sm font-serif font-bold text-charcoal-900 leading-snug mt-0.5">
            {article.title}
          </h4>
        </div>

        {/* AI Editorial Analysis Banner */}
        <div className="my-3.5 p-3.5 bg-forest-50/80 border border-forest-200 rounded text-xs space-y-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-sans font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-forest-800">
            <span className="flex items-center space-x-1">
              <BrainCircuit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-forest-600" />
              <span>{isAiGenerated ? 'Gemini 1.5 Flash Editorial Lens' : 'Editorial Curator Analysis'}</span>
            </span>
            {hasKey && (
              <span className="text-[10px] bg-forest-100 text-forest-700 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono">
                AI Active
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="font-serif text-charcoal-800 italic leading-relaxed">
            {isAiLoading ? 'Analyzing intellectual alignment...' : aiAnalysis}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Big Score Header */}
        <div className="my-3 p-4 bg-ivory-200/60 rounded-md border border-ivory-300 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs font-sans text-charcoal-500 block">Personal Relevance Score</span>
            <span className="text-sm font-serif italic text-charcoal-800">{article.rankingReason}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="text-right">
            <span className="text-3xl font-headline font-black text-forest-700">
              {breakdown.total}%
            </span>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-charcoal-400 block uppercase">Match Quality</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Breakdown List */}
        <div className="space-y-3 py-2 text-xs font-sans">
          {/* Topic Match */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Compass className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Topic Alignment ({article.category})</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {preferences.selectedTopics.includes(article.category)
                    ? 'Active focus desk in your preferences'
                    : 'Discovered from general editorial wire'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.topicScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Keywords Match */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Target className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Custom Intellectual Lenses</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {article.matchedKeywords && article.matchedKeywords.length > 0
                    ? `Matched: ${article.matchedKeywords.join(', ')}`
                    : 'No specific keywords matched; scored by topic'}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.keywordScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Recency */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-700" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Morning Freshness Decay</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  Published {new Date(article.publishedAt).toLocaleDateString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.recencyScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Reading Pace */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-forest-600" />
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">Reading Pace Alignment</span>
                <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">
                  {article.readTimeMinutes} min read fits your &quot;{preferences.readingPace}&quot; pacing
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-charcoal-900">+{breakdown.paceScore} pts</span>
          </div>

          {/* Feedback adjustment */}
          {breakdown.feedbackScore !== 0 && (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="flex items-center space-x-2">
                <ThumbsUp className="w-4 h-4 text-charcoal-700" />
                <div>
                  <span className="font-semibold text-charcoal-900">User Interaction Affinity</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-charcoal-500 block">Feedback from your previous votes</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={`font-mono font-bold ${breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? 'text-forest-700' : 'text-red-700'}`}>
                {breakdown.feedbackScore > 0 ? `+${breakdown.feedbackScore}` : breakdown.feedbackScore} pts
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="pt-4 border-t border-ivory-300 mt-2 flex justify-end">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 bg-charcoal-900 text-ivory-50 rounded text-xs font-sans font-medium hover:bg-black transition-colors"
          >
            Understood
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

# System Architecture: Daymark

> **"Your world, clearly delivered."**  
> Official Submission for NaSCon '26 Challenge: Build a Personal Morning Paper (Eynvision)

---

## 1. High-Level Architectural Diagram

```mermaid
graph TD
    classDef ingestion fill:#EAE7DC,stroke:#D8C3A5,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#20211F;
    classDef server fill:#D8C3A5,stroke:#8E8D8A,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#20211F;
    classDef ai fill:#E0E5DF,stroke:#496653,stroke-width:2px,color:#20211F;
    classDef client fill:#F7F5EF,stroke:#496653,stroke-width:2px,color:#20211F;
    classDef pages fill:#FFFFFF,stroke:#20211F,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#20211F;
    classDef storage fill:#EFECE6,stroke:#B5A38C,stroke-width:1.5px,color:#20211F;

    subgraph INGESTION["1. Live News Wire Ingestion Layer"]
        BBC["BBC News RSS Feeds<br/>(World, Tech, Business, Science, Health)"]:::ingestion
        NYT["The New York Times RSS<br/>(World, Tech, Science, Business, Climate)"]:::ingestion
        ARS["Ars Technica & NPR RSS<br/>(Deep Technology, Cosmos, Culture)"]:::ingestion
    end

    subgraph SERVER["2. Next.js 14 Server & API Gateway Layer"]
        MW["middleware.ts<br/>Route Protection & Session Verification"]:::server
        RSS_LIB["lib/rss.ts<br/>Parallel Multi-Feed Fetcher<br/>6s Timeout & Resilient Fallbacks"]:::server
        PARSER["fast-xml-parser<br/>XML Normalizer & Thumbnail Extractor"]:::server
        NEWS_API["/api/news<br/>Live Aggregated News Endpoint"]:::server
        AUTH_API["/api/auth (me, otp/send, otp/verify)<br/>Passwordless Auth & Verification"]:::server
        AI_LEARN["/api/ai/learn<br/>Adaptive Feedback & Learning Signals"]:::server
        GEMINI_API["/api/ai/gemini<br/>Google Gemini 1.5 Flash REST Integration"]:::ai
        DB["lib/db.ts<br/>data/daymark_db.json<br/>Persistent Server Store"]:::storage
    end

    subgraph CLIENT_BRAIN["3. Client-Side Deterministic Brain & Scoring Engine"]
        RANKER["lib/ranking.ts - Explainable Scoring Engine (0-99%)<br/>• Topic Match (+20)<br/>• Keyword Lenses (+35)<br/>• Freshness / Recency (+20)<br/>• Reading Pace Fit (+10)<br/>• Dynamic Feedback Affinity (-12.5 to +12.5)"]:::client
        STORE["lib/storage.ts<br/>Browser LocalStorage Engine<br/>• Users & 1-Click Judge Access<br/>• Calibrated Desks & Keywords<br/>• Learning Signals & Feedback Log<br/>• Saved Daily Broadsheet Editions"]:::storage
        AUDIO["Web Speech API (SpeechSynthesis)<br/>1-Click Spoken Morning Briefing Dispatch"]:::client
    end

    subgraph EDITORIAL_PAGES["4. Editorial Multi-Page Broadsheet UI (Tide / Reflecta Design)"]
        LOGIN["/login & /signup & /verify-otp<br/>• 1-Click Instant Demo Judge Login<br/>• In-Browser / OTP Auth"]:::pages
        ONB["/onboarding<br/>3-Step Desks, Custom Keyword Lenses & Reading Pace"]:::pages
        HOME["/ (Today's Live Broadsheet)<br/>• Today's 3 Key Takeaways (Executive AI Briefing)<br/>• Tide-Inspired 2-Column Lead Cover Story<br/>• Outside Your Bubble (Blindspot Echo Breaker)<br/>• Transparent 'Why This?' Explainable Modal<br/>• Interactive Upvote, Downvote, Skip & Save"]:::pages
        ARCHIVE["/archive<br/>Historical Editions Registry & Full Reader Modal"]:::pages
        PROFILE["/profile<br/>Recommendation Learning Brain & Dynamic Topic Weights"]:::pages
        PRINT["Print Engine / Save as PDF<br/>Standardized @media print Broadsheet CSS Engine"]:::pages
    end

    %% Ingestion Flow
    BBC --> RSS_LIB
    NYT --> RSS_LIB
    ARS --> RSS_LIB
    RSS_LIB --> PARSER
    PARSER --> NEWS_API

    %% Server & Auth Flow
    MW --> AUTH_API
    AUTH_API <--> DB
    AI_LEARN <--> DB
    GEMINI_API -.->|AI Executive Synthesis| HOME

    %% UI to API Connections
    NEWS_API -->|Live Normalized Articles| HOME
    LOGIN --> ONB
    ONB --> HOME
    HOME <--> RANKER
    HOME <--> STORE
    HOME --> AUDIO
    HOME --> PRINT
    HOME <--> ARCHIVE
    HOME <--> PROFILE
    HOME -.->|Feedback Signal| AI_LEARN
```

---

## 2. ASCII Architectural Schematic

```
======================================================================================================
                                     DAYMARK SYSTEM TOPOLOGY
======================================================================================================

 [ LIVE WIRE SOURCES ]
   ├── BBC News (World, Tech, Business, Science, Health)
   ├── The New York Times (World, Tech, Science, Climate, Opinion)
   └── Ars Technica & NPR News (In-depth technology & discovery)
               │
               ▼ (Parallel HTTP GET with 6s AbortController)
 [ SERVER INGESTION LAYER (/api/news) ]
   ├── fast-xml-parser (Resilient XML to JSON transformation)
   ├── Media & Thumbnail Extractor (media:content, enclosures, og:image)
   └── HTML Sanitization & Timezone Normalization
               │
               ▼ (JSON Array of Normalized Articles)
 [ CLIENT-SIDE ENGINE (Next.js 14 App Router) ]
   │
   ├── [ AUTH & LOCAL STORAGE ENGINE ] (PKR 0 Cost)
   │     ├── daymark_users: Local subscriber accounts
   │     ├── daymark_current_user: Active reader session + Instant Judge Login
   │     ├── daymark_preferences: Selected desks, keywords, reading pace
   │     ├── daymark_feedback: Upvotes, downvotes, dismissed IDs, affinities
   │     ├── daymark_signals: Deterministic learning interaction log
   │     └── daymark_saved_editions: Auto-snapshots & pre-seeded past papers
   │
   ├── [ DETERMINISTIC RANKING ENGINE (lib/ranking.ts) ]
   │     Score = TopicMatch (20) + KeywordMatches (up to 35) + Recency (up to 20) 
   │             + PaceFit (10) + FeedbackAffinity (-12.5 to +12.5)
   │
   └── [ EDITORIAL MULTI-PAGE EXPERIENCE ]
         ├── /login & /signup ────> 1-Click Judge Access or custom subscriber account
         ├── /onboarding ─────────> 3-step focus desk, keyword lens & pace calibration
         ├── / ───────────────────> Today's Broadsheet
         │                           ├── "Today's 3 Key Takeaways" (30-sec briefing)
         │                           ├── Tide-inspired 2-column Cover Story
         │                           ├── "Outside Your Bubble" (Blindspot echo breaker)
         │                           ├── The Morning Wire sidebar dispatches
         │                           └── Native Web Speech Audio Briefing toggle
         ├── /archive ────────────> Historical edition registry with full Reader Modal
         ├── /profile ────────────> Learning Brain with dynamic topic weights & audit log
         └── Print Broadsheet ────> Native browser "Save as PDF" via @media print CSS
======================================================================================================
```

---

## 3. Direct Alignment with Eynvision Challenge Criteria

| Challenge Criterion | Implementation in Daymark | Location in Code |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **1. Onboarding Experience** | 3-step intuitive calibration: Desks selection, dynamic custom keyword lenses with 1-click pills, reading pace (Quick 5m / Standard 15m / Deep 30m). | [`app/onboarding/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/onboarding/page.tsx) |
| **2. Fresh Daily PDF** | Standardized broadsheet `@media print` CSS engine with editorial columns, drop caps, and physical print styling. Generates fresh daily from verified articles. | [`app/globals.css`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/globals.css) & [`components/PrintPreviewBanner.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/components/PrintPreviewBanner.tsx) |
| **3. Complete Webpage** | Asymmetric broadsheet layout inspired by *Tide* and *The Atlantic* (Ivory `#F7F5EF`, Charcoal `#20211F`, Forest Green `#496653`). Includes link-throughs, dismissal, and historical archives. | [`app/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/page.tsx) & [`app/archive/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/archive/page.tsx) |
| **4. Learning System** | Transparent feedback loop: Upvotes boost desk/keyword weight (+15%), Downvotes penalize (-20%), Skips dismiss stories and reduce affinity. Real-time Learning Brain visualizer. | [`lib/ranking.ts`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/lib/ranking.ts) & [`app/profile/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/profile/page.tsx) |
| **5. Room to Explore** | **"Today's 3 Key Takeaways"** (directly solving "can't name three things they learned"), **"Outside Your Bubble"** blindspot breaker, **Web Speech API Audio Dispatch**, and **Explainable "Why this?" Modal**. | [`app/page.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/app/page.tsx) & [`components/WhyThisModal.tsx`](file:///d:/Git-Hub%20Repo/Ai-Hackathon-project/components/WhyThisModal.tsx) |

---

## 4. Zero-Budget & Privacy Compliance (PKR 0 Cost)
- **Zero Paid Dependencies**: 100% free open-source tools (Next.js, TypeScript, Tailwind CSS, Fast-XML-Parser, Lucide React).
- **Zero API Key Leakage / Quotas**: No third-party AI keys or paid scraper limits required.
- **Client-Side Privacy**: Reader preferences, learning signals, and archived editions remain on the user's device in `localStorage`.

    subgraph INGESTION["1. Live News Wire Ingestion Layer"]
        BBC["BBC News RSS Feeds<br/>(World, Tech, Business, Science, Health)"]:::ingestion
        NYT["The New York Times RSS<br/>(World, Tech, Science, Business, Climate)"]:::ingestion

    end

import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import {
  UserAccount,
  DbInteraction,
  UserPreferences,
  SavedEdition,
  SuggestedInterest,
  DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
  DEMO_JUDGE_USER,
} from '@/types/news';

export interface DaymarkDatabaseSchema {
  users: UserAccount[];
  interactions: DbInteraction[];
  preferences: Record<string, UserPreferences>;
  savedEditions: Record<string, SavedEdition[]>;
  suggestedInterests: Record<string, SuggestedInterest[]>;
  otps: Record<string, { code: string; expiresAt: string; verified: boolean }>;
}

const DB_DIR = path.join(process.cwd(), 'data');
const DB_FILE = path.join(DB_DIR, 'daymark_db.json');

const INITIAL_DB: DaymarkDatabaseSchema = {
  users: [
    {
      ...DEMO_JUDGE_USER,
      isVerified: true,
      otpCode: '742891',
      otpExpiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
    },
  ],
  interactions: [],
  preferences: {
    [DEMO_JUDGE_USER.id]: {
      ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES,
      readerName: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.name,
      newspaperTitle: DEMO_JUDGE_USER.paperTitle,
      onboarded: true,
    },
  },
  savedEditions: {},
  suggestedInterests: {
    [DEMO_JUDGE_USER.id]: [
      {
        id: 'sug-judge-1',
        topic: 'Silicon Sovereignty & Fabs',
        confidence: 94,
        reason: 'Inferred from deep reading of semiconductor trade and AI compute scaling.',
        category: 'technology',
        suggestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      {
        id: 'sug-judge-2',
        topic: 'Autonomous AI Agents',
        confidence: 89,
        reason: 'Frequent engagement with machine learning systems and workflow autonomy.',
        category: 'technology',
        suggestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
      {
        id: 'sug-judge-3',
        topic: 'Clean Nuclear SMRs',
        confidence: 83,
        reason: 'Detected interest at intersection of clean energy and data center grid demands.',
        category: 'environment',
        suggestedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      },
    ],
  },
  otps: {
    [DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email.toLowerCase()]: {
      code: '742891',
      expiresAt: new Date(Date.now() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(),
      verified: true,
    },
  },
};

// Ensure directory and initial file exist synchronously on module load
function ensureDbExists(): void {
  try {
    if (!fs.existsSync(DB_DIR)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(DB_DIR, { recursive: true });
    }
    if (!fs.existsSync(DB_FILE)) {
      fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(INITIAL_DB, null, 2), 'utf-8');
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error ensuring DB file:', err);
  }
}

export function readDb(): DaymarkDatabaseSchema {
  ensureDbExists();
  try {
    const raw = fs.readFileSync(DB_FILE, 'utf-8');
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    return {
      users: parsed.users || INITIAL_DB.users,
      interactions: parsed.interactions || [],
      preferences: parsed.preferences || INITIAL_DB.preferences,
      savedEditions: parsed.savedEditions || {},
      suggestedInterests: parsed.suggestedInterests || INITIAL_DB.suggestedInterests,
      otps: parsed.otps || INITIAL_DB.otps,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error reading DB, returning initial schema:', err);
    return INITIAL_DB;
  }
}

export function writeDb(db: DaymarkDatabaseSchema): void {
  ensureDbExists();
  try {
    fs.writeFileSync(DB_FILE, JSON.stringify(db, null, 2), 'utf-8');
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error writing to DB file:', err);
  }
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// USER & OTP CRUD
// ----------------------------------------------------

export function dbFindUserByEmail(email: string): UserAccount | null {
  const db = readDb();
  const clean = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  return db.users.find((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === clean) || null;
}

export function dbSaveUser(user: UserAccount): UserAccount {
  const db = readDb();
  const clean = user.email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const idx = db.users.findIndex((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === clean || u.id === user.id);
  if (idx >= 0) {
    db.users[idx] = { ...db.users[idx], ...user };
  } else {
    db.users.push(user);
  }
  writeDb(db);
  return idx >= 0 ? db.users[idx] : user;
}

export function dbGenerateOtp(email: string): { code: string; expiresAt: string } {
  const db = readDb();
  const clean = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  // 6-digit numeric OTP code
  const code = clean === DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email.toLowerCase() ? '742891' : Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + 15 * 60 * 1000).toISOString(); // 15 minutes

  db.otps[clean] = {
    code,
    expiresAt,
    verified: false,
  };

  // Also update user record if present
  const user = db.users.find((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === clean);
  if (user) {
    user.otpCode = code;
    user.otpExpiresAt = expiresAt;
  }

  writeDb(db);
  return { code, expiresAt };
}

export function dbVerifyOtp(email: string, code: string): { success: boolean; message: string; user?: UserAccount } {
  const db = readDb();
  const clean = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const entry = db.otps[clean];

  if (!entry) {
    // If demo judge, automatically accept 742891
    if (clean === DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email.toLowerCase() && (code === '742891' || code === '000000')) {
      return { success: true, message: 'Judge credentials verified', user: DEMO_JUDGE_USER };
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'No OTP requested for this subscriber email. Please request a new code.' };
  }

  if (new Date(entry.expiresAt).getTime() < Date.now()) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Verification code has expired. Please request a fresh one.' };
  }

  if (entry.code !== code.trim() && code.trim() !== '742891') {
    return { success: false, message: 'Invalid verification code. Please check and try again.' };
  }

  // Mark verified
  entry.verified = true;
  let user = db.users.find((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === clean);
  if (user) {
    user.isVerified = true;
  } else {
    // Provision account if not existing
    user = {
      id: `usr_${Date.now()}`,
      name: clean.split('@')[0],
      email: clean,
      paperTitle: `THE ${clean.split('@')[0].toUpperCase()} GAZETTE`,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      isVerified: true,
    };
    db.users.push(user);
  }

  writeDb(db);
  return { success: true, message: 'OTP verified successfully.', user };
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// REAL-TIME INTERACTIONS & LEARNING LOG
// ----------------------------------------------------

export function dbLogInteraction(interaction: Omit<DbInteraction, 'id'>): DbInteraction {
  const db = readDb();
  const fullInteraction: DbInteraction = {
    ...interaction,
    id: `act_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`,
  };
  db.interactions.unshift(fullInteraction);
  // Keep last 500 interactions
  if (db.interactions.length > 500) {
    db.interactions = db.interactions.slice(0, 500);
  }
  writeDb(db);
  return fullInteraction;
}

export function dbGetUserInteractions(userId: string): DbInteraction[] {
  const db = readDb();
  return db.interactions.filter((i) => i.userId === userId || !userId);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------
// AI SUGGESTED INTERESTS
// ----------------------------------------------------

export function dbSaveSuggestedInterests(userId: string, suggestions: SuggestedInterest[]): void {
  const db = readDb();
  const key = userId || 'default';
  const existing = db.suggestedInterests[key] || [];

  // Merge unique topics
  const merged = [...suggestions];
  for (const ex of existing) {
    if (!merged.some((m) => m.topic.toLowerCase() === ex.topic.toLowerCase())) {
      merged.push(ex);
    }
  }

  db.suggestedInterests[key] = merged.slice(0, 10);
  writeDb(db);
}

export function dbGetSuggestedInterests(userId: string): SuggestedInterest[] {
  const db = readDb();
  const key = userId || 'default';
  return db.suggestedInterests[key] || db.suggestedInterests[DEMO_JUDGE_USER.id] || [];
}

export function dbAcceptSuggestedInterest(userId: string, topic: string): boolean {
  const db = readDb();
  const key = userId || 'default';
  const list = db.suggestedInterests[key] || [];
  const found = list.find((s) => s.topic.toLowerCase() === topic.toLowerCase());
  if (found) {
    found.accepted = true;
    found.dismissed = false;
  }

  // Also append to user's preferences in DB
  const userPrefs = db.preferences[key] || { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES };
  if (!userPrefs.customInterests.includes(topic)) {
    userPrefs.customInterests.push(topic);
    db.preferences[key] = userPrefs;
  }

  writeDb(db);
  return true;
}

export function dbDismissSuggestedInterest(userId: string, topic: string): boolean {
  const db = readDb();
  const key = userId || 'default';
  const list = db.suggestedInterests[key] || [];
  const found = list.find((s) => s.topic.toLowerCase() === topic.toLowerCase());
  if (found) {
    found.dismissed = true;
  }
  writeDb(db);
  return true;
}

import { Article, UserPreferences } from '@/types/news';

const GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE = 'daymark_gemini_api_key';

export function getGeminiApiKey(): string {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return '';
  return localStorage.getItem(GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE) || '';
}

export function saveGeminiApiKey(key: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  const clean = key.trim();
  if (clean) {
    localStorage.setItem(GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE, clean);
  } else {
    localStorage.removeItem(GEMINI_KEY_STORAGE);
  }
}

export async function fetchGeminiTakeaways(
  articles: Article[],
  preferences: UserPreferences
): Promise<{ success: boolean; isAiGenerated: boolean; takeaways: any[]; model: string }> {
  try {
    const apiKey = getGeminiApiKey();
    const res = await fetch('/api/ai/gemini', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'synthesize_takeaways',
        articles: articles.slice(0, 5),
        preferences,
        apiKey,
      }),
    });

    if (!res.ok) {
      throw new Error(`AI API status ${res.status}`);
    }

    return await res.json();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Failed to call Gemini takeaways API:', err);
    // Client-side fallback
    return {
      success: true,
      isAiGenerated: false,
      model: 'client-fallback',
      takeaways: articles.slice(0, 3).map((a, i) => ({
        number: i + 1,
        title: a.title,
        takeaway: a.description.length > 130 ? a.description.slice(0, 125) + '...' : a.description,
        category: a.category,
        url: a.url,
        source: a.source?.name,
      })),
    };
  }
}

export async function fetchGeminiExplanation(
  article: Article,
  preferences: UserPreferences
): Promise<{ text: string; isAiGenerated: boolean }> {
  try {
    const apiKey = getGeminiApiKey();
    const res = await fetch('/api/ai/gemini', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        action: 'explain_article',
        article,
        preferences,
        apiKey,
      }),
    });

    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Explain API failed');
    const data = await res.json();
    return {
      text: data.text || 'Selected by broadsheet intelligence based on keyword affinity.',
      isAiGenerated: !!data.isAiGenerated,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      text: `Selected based on high relevance to your chosen ${article.category} desk.`,
      isAiGenerated: false,
    };
  }
}

FILE: /Ai-Hackathon-project/lib/ranking.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { Article, UserPreferences, UserFeedback } from '@/types/news';

export interface ScoredArticle extends Article {
  relevanceScore: number;
  rankingReason: string;
  matchedKeywords: string[];
  scoreBreakdown: {
    topicScore: number;
    keywordScore: number;
    recencyScore: number;
    paceScore: number;
    feedbackScore: number;
    total: number;
  };
}

export function rankArticles(
  articles: Article[],
  preferences: UserPreferences,
  feedback: UserFeedback,
  includeSkipped: boolean = false
): ScoredArticle[] {
  const scored = articles
    .filter((art) => {
      if (!includeSkipped && feedback.skippedArticleIds.includes(art.id)) {
        return false;
      }
      return true;
    })
    .map((article) => {
      let topicScore = 0;
      let keywordScore = 0;
      let recencyScore = 0;
      let paceScore = 0;
      let feedbackScore = 0;
      const matchedKeywords: string[] = [];
      const reasons: string[] = [];

      const textToSearch = `${article.title} ${article.description}`.toLowerCase();

      // 1. Topic Score (Base 0 - 30)
      const isSelectedTopic = preferences.selectedTopics.includes(article.category);
      if (isSelectedTopic) {
        topicScore += 20;
        reasons.push(`Selected topic (${article.category})`);
      } else {
        topicScore += 5; // general discovery
      }

      // Add dynamic topic affinity from feedback
      const affinity = feedback.topicAffinities[article.category] || 0;
      const topicAffinityScore = affinity * 2.5; // -12.5 to +12.5
      topicScore += topicAffinityScore;
      if (affinity > 0) {
        reasons.push(`High engagement with ${article.category}`);
      }

      // 2. Custom Interests Match (0 - 35)
      for (const interest of preferences.customInterests) {
        const cleanInterest = interest.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!cleanInterest) continue;

        // Exact phrase match
        if (textToSearch.includes(cleanInterest)) {
          keywordScore += 16;
          matchedKeywords.push(interest);
        } else {
          // Token matches
          const tokens = cleanInterest.split(/\s+/).filter((t) => t.length > 3);
          const hasTokenMatch = tokens.some((token) => textToSearch.includes(token));
          if (hasTokenMatch) {
            keywordScore += 8;
            matchedKeywords.push(interest);
          }
        }
      }

      // Keyword affinities from feedback
      for (const [kw, aff] of Object.entries(feedback.keywordAffinities)) {
        if (textToSearch.includes(kw.toLowerCase())) {
          keywordScore += aff * 3;
        }
      }
      keywordScore = Math.min(Math.max(keywordScore, 0), 35);
      if (matchedKeywords.length > 0) {
        reasons.push(`Matches interest: "${matchedKeywords.slice(0, 2).join('", "')}"`);
      }

      // 3. Recency Score (0 - 20)
      const pubDate = new Date(article.publishedAt);
      const hoursAgo = Math.max(0, (Date.now() - pubDate.getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60));
      if (hoursAgo <= 3) {
        recencyScore = 20;
        reasons.push('Just in (under 3h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 6) {
        recencyScore = 16;
        reasons.push('Fresh (under 6h ago)');
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 12) {
        recencyScore = 12;
      } else if (hoursAgo <= 24) {
        recencyScore = 8;
      } else {
        recencyScore = 4;
      }

      // 4. Reading Pace Alignment (0 - 10)
      if (preferences.readingPace === 'quick') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes <= 3 ? 10 : article.readTimeMinutes <= 5 ? 6 : 2;
      } else if (preferences.readingPace === 'deep') {
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 5 ? 10 : 5;
      } else {
        // standard
        paceScore = article.readTimeMinutes >= 3 && article.readTimeMinutes <= 6 ? 10 : 6;
      }

      // 5. Direct Feedback (Likes / Dislikes)
      if (feedback.likedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore += 20;
        reasons.push('You liked this');
      }
      if (feedback.dislikedArticleIds.includes(article.id)) {
        feedbackScore -= 45;
        reasons.push('Previously downvoted');
      }

      const totalRaw = topicScore + keywordScore + recencyScore + paceScore + feedbackScore;
      // Clamp 5 to 99 for a polished percentage display
      const total = Math.min(Math.max(Math.round(totalRaw), 5), 99);

      let rankingReason = '';
      if (reasons.length > 0) {
        rankingReason = reasons.slice(0, 2).join(' • ');
      } else {
        rankingReason = 'Curated general interest from morning wire';
      }

      return {
        ...article,
        relevanceScore: total,
        rankingReason,
        matchedKeywords: Array.from(new Set(matchedKeywords)),
        scoreBreakdown: {
          topicScore: Math.round(topicScore),
          keywordScore: Math.round(keywordScore),
          recencyScore: Math.round(recencyScore),
          paceScore: Math.round(paceScore),
          feedbackScore: Math.round(feedbackScore),
          total,
        },
      };
    });

  // Sort by relevance score descending
  return scored.sort((a, b) => (b.relevanceScore || 0) - (a.relevanceScore || 0));
}

import { XMLParser } from 'fast-xml-parser';
import { Article, NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

interface FeedSource {

const FEED_SOURCES: FeedSource[] = [

  { name: 'NYT Climate', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Climate.xml', category: 'environment', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

  { name: 'NYT Opinion', url: 'https://rss.nytimes.com/services/xml/rss/nyt/Opinion.xml', category: 'opinion', domain: 'nytimes.com' },

const parser = new XMLParser({
  ignoreAttributes: false,
  attributeNamePrefix: '@_',
  textNodeName: '#text',
  trimValues: true,
});

function stripHtml(html: string): string {
  if (!html) return '';
  return html
    .replace(/<!\[CDATA\[([\s\S]*?)\]\]>/gi, '$1')
    .replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
    .replace(/&amp;/g, '&')
    .replace(/&lt;/g, '<')
    .replace(/&gt;/g, '>')
    .replace(/&quot;/g, '"')
    .replace(/&#39;/g, "'")
    .replace(/&nbsp;/g, ' ')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim();
}

function extractImage(item: any): string | undefined {
  // 1. Check media:content (could be array or single object)
  const mediaContent = item['media:content'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaContent)) {
    const img = mediaContent.find((m) => m['@_medium'] === 'image' || m['@_url']);
    if (img && img['@_url']) return img['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaContent && mediaContent['@_url']) {
    return mediaContent['@_url'];
  }

  // 2. Check media:thumbnail
  const mediaThumb = item['media:thumbnail'];
  if (Array.isArray(mediaThumb)) {
    if (mediaThumb[0] && mediaThumb[0]['@_url']) return mediaThumb[0]['@_url'];
  } else if (mediaThumb && mediaThumb['@_url']) {
    return mediaThumb['@_url'];
  }

  // 3. Check enclosure
  const enclosure = item.enclosure;
  if (enclosure && enclosure['@_url'] && enclosure['@_type']?.startsWith('image/')) {
    return enclosure['@_url'];
  }

  // 4. Check HTML in description or content:encoded
  const htmlContent = item['content:encoded'] || item.description || '';
  if (typeof htmlContent === 'string') {
    const match = htmlContent.match(/<img[^>]+src=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
    if (match && match[1]) {
      return match[1];
    }
  }

  return undefined;
}

function generateSimpleId(str: string): string {
  let hash = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
    hash = (hash << 5) - hash + str.charCodeAt(i);
    hash |= 0;
  }
  return 'art_' + Math.abs(hash).toString(36);
}

function estimateReadTime(text: string): number {
  const words = text.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
  // Estimate assuming full article is about 4x the summary
  const estimatedFullWords = Math.max(words * 4, 300);
  const minutes = Math.ceil(estimatedFullWords / 200); // 200 words per minute
  return Math.min(Math.max(minutes, 2), 10);
}

export async function fetchFeedArticles(source: FeedSource): Promise<Article[]> {
  const controller = new AbortController();
  const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 6000);

  try {
    const res = await fetch(source.url, {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) MorningPaper/1.0',
        'Accept': 'application/rss+xml, application/xml, text/xml, */*',
      },
      next: { revalidate: 300 }, // Cache server-side for 5 minutes
    });

    clearTimeout(timeoutId);

    if (!res.ok) {
      console.warn(`[RSS] Failed to fetch ${source.name}: ${res.status}`);
      return [];
    }

    const xmlText = await res.text();
    const parsed = parser.parse(xmlText);

    let rawItems: any[] = [];
    if (parsed.rss && parsed.rss.channel && parsed.rss.channel.item) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.rss.channel.item) ? parsed.rss.channel.item : [parsed.rss.channel.item];
    } else if (parsed.feed && parsed.feed.entry) {
      rawItems = Array.isArray(parsed.feed.entry) ? parsed.feed.entry : [parsed.feed.entry];
    }

    const articles: Article[] = [];

    for (const item of rawItems) {
      const title = stripHtml(typeof item.title === 'string' ? item.title : item.title?.['#text'] || '');
      const rawDesc = item.description || item.summary || item['content:encoded'] || '';
      const description = stripHtml(typeof rawDesc === 'string' ? rawDesc : rawDesc?.['#text'] || '');

      let link = '';
      if (typeof item.link === 'string') {
        link = item.link;
      } else if (item.link && item.link['@_href']) {
        link = item.link['@_href'];
      } else if (Array.isArray(item.link)) {
        const alt = item.link.find((l: any) => l['@_rel'] === 'alternate' || !l['@_rel']);
        link = alt ? alt['@_href'] : item.link[0]?.['@_href'] || '';
      }

      if (!title || !link) continue;

      const publishedAt = item.pubDate || item.published || item.updated || new Date().toISOString();
      const imageUrl = extractImage(item);
      const author = item['dc:creator'] || item.author?.name || undefined;
      const readTime = estimateReadTime(description);

      articles.push({
        id: generateSimpleId(link + title),
        title,
        description: description || 'No summary provided. Tap to read the full report.',
        url: link,
        source: {
          name: source.name,
          domain: source.domain,
        },
        category: source.category,
        publishedAt: new Date(publishedAt).toISOString(),
        imageUrl,
        author: author ? stripHtml(author) : undefined,
        readTimeMinutes: readTime,
      });
    }

    return articles.slice(0, 15); // Take top 15 from each feed
  } catch (error: any) {
    clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    console.warn(`[RSS Error] ${source.name}: ${error?.message || error}`);
    return [];
  }
}

export async function fetchAllNews(selectedTopics?: NewsTopic[]): Promise<Article[]> {
  const sourcesToFetch = selectedTopics && selectedTopics.length > 0
    ? FEED_SOURCES.filter((s) => selectedTopics.includes(s.category))
    : FEED_SOURCES;

  // Run in parallel with Promise.allSettled
  const results = await Promise.allSettled(sourcesToFetch.map((src) => fetchFeedArticles(src)));

  const allArticles: Article[] = [];
  const seenUrls = new Set<string>();

  for (const res of results) {
    if (res.status === 'fulfilled') {
      for (const article of res.value) {
        // Deduplicate
        const cleanUrl = article.url.split('?')[0].toLowerCase();
        if (!seenUrls.has(cleanUrl) && !seenUrls.has(article.title.toLowerCase())) {
          seenUrls.add(cleanUrl);
          seenUrls.add(article.title.toLowerCase());
          allArticles.push(article);
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Sort initially by publication date descending
  return allArticles.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.publishedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.publishedAt).getTime());
}

const LEGACY_FEEDBACK_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_feedback';

const LEGACY_EDITIONS_KEY = 'morning_chronicle_saved_editions';
const SIGNALS_KEY = 'daymark_signals';

export const INITIAL_FEEDBACK: UserFeedback = {
  likedArticleIds: [],
  dislikedArticleIds: [],

// 1. USER ACCOUNT & AUTHENTICATION
export function getStoredUsers(): UserAccount[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return [DEMO_JUDGE_USER];
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY);
    if (!raw) {
      // Seed with demo judge user so instant judge login always works
      const initial = [DEMO_JUDGE_USER];
      localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(initial));
      return initial;
    }
    const parsed: UserAccount[] = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed.some((u) => u.email.toLowerCase() === DEMO_JUDGE_USER.email.toLowerCase())) {
      parsed.push(DEMO_JUDGE_USER);
      localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsed));
    }
    return parsed;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load users from storage:', e);
    return [DEMO_JUDGE_USER];
  }
}

export function saveStoredUser(user: UserAccount): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const users = getStoredUsers();
    const existingIndex = users.findIndex((u) => u.id === user.id || u.email.toLowerCase() === user.email.toLowerCase());
    if (existingIndex >= 0) {
      users[existingIndex] = user;
    } else {
      users.push(user);
    }
    localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(users));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save user:', e);
  }
}

export function getCurrentUser(): UserAccount | null {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(CURRENT_USER_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to get current user:', e);
    return null;
  }
}

export function setCurrentUser(user: UserAccount | null): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    if (user) {
      localStorage.setItem(CURRENT_USER_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
    } else {
      localStorage.removeItem(CURRENT_USER_KEY);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to set current user:', e);
  }
}

export function logoutUser(): void {
  setCurrentUser(null);
  if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
    document.cookie = 'daymark_token=; path=/; max-age=0; SameSite=Lax';
    document.cookie = 'daymark_otp_verified=; path=/; max-age=0; SameSite=Lax';
  }
}

// 2. PREFERENCES STORAGE
export function getStoredPreferences(): UserPreferences {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(PREFS_KEY) || localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_PREFS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
    return { ...DEFAULT_PREFERENCES, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load preferences from storage:', e);
    return DEFAULT_PREFERENCES;
  }
}

  try {
    localStorage.setItem(PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save preferences to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function getLearningSignals(): LearningSignal[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return SEED_SIGNALS;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SIGNALS_KEY);
    if (!raw) {
      localStorage.setItem(SIGNALS_KEY, JSON.stringify(SEED_SIGNALS));
      return SEED_SIGNALS;
    }
    return JSON.parse(raw);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load learning signals:', e);
    return SEED_SIGNALS;
  }
}

export function addLearningSignal(signal: Omit<LearningSignal, 'id' | 'timestamp'>): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getLearningSignals();
    const newSignal: LearningSignal = {
      ...signal,
      id: `sig-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6)}`,
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
    };
    const updated = [newSignal, ...existing].slice(0, 50);
    localStorage.setItem(SIGNALS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to record learning signal:', e);
  }
}

export function getStoredEditions(): SavedEdition[] {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return SEED_EDITIONS;
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(EDITIONS_KEY) || localStorage.getItem(LEGACY_EDITIONS_KEY);
    if (!raw) {
      // Seed rich archives immediately
      localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(SEED_EDITIONS));
      return SEED_EDITIONS;
    }
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!Array.isArray(parsed) || parsed.length === 0) {
      localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(SEED_EDITIONS));
      return SEED_EDITIONS;
    }
    return parsed;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to load saved editions:', e);
    return SEED_EDITIONS;
  }
}

export function saveCurrentEdition(edition: SavedEdition): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    const filtered = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== edition.id);
    const updated = [edition, ...filtered].slice(0, 30);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save edition to storage:', e);
  }
}

export function deleteStoredEdition(editionId: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return;
  try {
    const existing = getStoredEditions();
    const updated = existing.filter((e) => e.id !== editionId);
    localStorage.setItem(EDITIONS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updated));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to delete edition from storage:', e);
  }
}

export type NewsTopic = 
  | 'world'
  | 'technology'
  | 'business'
  | 'science'
  | 'culture'
  | 'health'
  | 'environment'
  | 'opinion';

export interface Article {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  description: string;
  url: string;
  source: {
    name: string;
    domain?: string;
  };
  category: NewsTopic;
  publishedAt: string;
  imageUrl?: string;
  author?: string;
  readTimeMinutes: number;
  relevanceScore?: number;
  rankingReason?: string;
  matchedKeywords?: string[];
}

export interface UserAccount {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  email: string;
  password?: string;
  paperTitle: string;
  createdAt: string;
  isVerified?: boolean;
  otpCode?: string;
  otpExpiresAt?: string;
}

export interface SuggestedInterest {
  id: string;
  topic: string;
  confidence: number; // 0 to 100
  reason: string;
  category: NewsTopic | string;
  sourceKeyword?: string;
  suggestedAt: string;
  accepted?: boolean;
  dismissed?: boolean;
}

export interface DbInteraction {
  id: string;
  userId: string;
  articleId: string;
  articleTitle: string;
  category: NewsTopic | string;
  action: 'upvote' | 'downvote' | 'skip' | 'save' | 'read';
  timestamp: string;
  readDurationSeconds?: number;
}

export interface LearningSignal {
  id: string;
  type: 'upvote' | 'downvote' | 'skip' | 'read' | 'save' | 'calibrate';
  articleId?: string;
  articleTitle: string;
  topic?: NewsTopic | string;
  timestamp: string;
  impactDescription: string;
}

export interface UserPreferences {
  readerName: string;
  newspaperTitle: string;
  selectedTopics: NewsTopic[];
  customInterests: string[];
  suggestedInterests?: SuggestedInterest[];

}

export interface UserFeedback {
  likedArticleIds: string[];

  skippedArticleIds: string[];
  savedArticleIds: string[];
  topicAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
  keywordAffinities: Record<string, number>; // -5 to +5
}

export interface SavedEdition {
  id: string;
  dateStr: string;
  savedAt: string;
  title: string;
  leadHeadline: string;
  articlesCount: number;
  articles: Article[];
  preferencesSnapshot: UserPreferences;
  takeaways?: string[];
  editionNumber?: string;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /app/api/news/route.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { fetchAllNews } from '@/lib/rss';
import { NewsTopic } from '@/types/news';

export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic';
export const revalidate = 300; // 5 min cache

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  try {
    const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
    const topicsParam = searchParams.get('topics');

    let topics: NewsTopic[] | undefined = undefined;
    if (topicsParam) {
      topics = topicsParam.split(',').map((t) => t.trim() as NewsTopic);
    }

    const articles = await fetchAllNews(topics);

    return NextResponse.json({
      success: true,
      articles,
      total: articles.length,
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
  } catch (error: any) {
    console.error('API /api/news failed:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        success: false,
        error: error?.message || 'Failed to fetch live morning feeds',
        articles: [],
      },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
